“Yes.” Laurel put down her plate and got up to move into Rowan’s arms and get a hug. “You know me pretty well after all these years. And the amazing thing is you still put up with me.” 
“You got that right. Any time . . . anywhere . . . I’ll put up with you . . . repeatedly.” Rowan laughed and kissed Laurel. 
Mabel turned away from watching Rowan and Laurel, and looked at Maynard questioningly. 
“I know.” Maynard said when he caught Mabel’s look. “Try and get used to it . . . they act like this all the time.” 
Mabel looked back at Rowan and Laurel where they were still engrossed in their kiss. 
“They’re amazing.” Mabel commented quietly. 
“Welcome back.” Amelia’s voice filled Recon. “I hope you had a pleasant trip.”
“Yes, we did.” Rowan broke away from Laurel to answer. “Thank you.” 
“I’m delighted to hear it. Maynard told me last night that you were successful in getting some spaceships to help us. I’m already researching the best way to go about making a sling to capture the comet. I have a few more simulations to run, but we should be ready to construct the sling soon. In the meantime, Rodjer and I will be standing by in Lab when you get there.” 
“Thank you very much.” Rowan answered. He gathered up Laurel’s breakfast dishes as well as his own and sprinted back to put them in the Recycle Slot in the Food Dispenser. Then he hurried back to the front. Recon’s hatch was opening. They were home again. 

* * *

Juniper, Laurel, and Willow sat at the table adjacent to the dance floor area. They were looking off across the expanse of the dance floor to where they could see the horizon above the meadow trees. The horizon was crimson. They were talking and slowly sipping very dry white wine as night swiftly fell around them. Cooler night air began to circulate as stars started to pop into view over their heads. 
“So . . . Mabel’s turned out to be a pretty interesting place.” Juniper commented as she lifted her blonde hair up so the night air could cool the afternoon heat off her neck. “God . . . that breeze feels good.” 
“It’s nice and peaceful when the stars come out and the day cools off.” Willow agreed quietly. 
“Mabel’s was fascinating.” Laurel continued to tell them about the trip. “The perfect spot, especially if you like to watch people. And the way the surrounding tables would unexpectedly change into something new, it always made the view new and different. There were so many people having fun in various ways. We’ll have to visit there after we get the comet moved. You’d both love it.” 
“What’s Mabel like?” Juniper was curious. 
“He’s gorgeous . . . a downright breathtaking specimen of maleness.” Laurel said with conviction, and then she laughed. “He’s a bit of a rogue, too. He’s very full of himself.” 
“It sounds like he thinks he’s God’s gift to women.” Willow commented. 
“Not only that . . . but since he can turn into a woman at the drop of a hat . . . on occasion he thinks he’s God’s gift to men as well.” Laurel replied with a smile. 
“That sounds confusing.” Juniper laughed. “Was it strange being around him?” 
“In some ways ‘yes’ and in some ways ‘no’.” Laurel paused to pick her words carefully. “It was very strange for him to suddenly change from a man into a woman or vice versa. The change is a bit of a jolt. But the strangeness only lasts a moment. He comes on very strong no matter which guise he’s in. He’s so totally each persona, that you forget he could be anything else.” 
“Wow.” Juniper said with a grin. “It sounds like he may be many things . . . but boring isn’t one of them.” 
“How right you are.” Laurel laughed. “How right you are.”
“While you all were gone to Mabel’s, we found out something very interesting.” Juniper spoke after a pause in the conversation. “Amelia helped us run some data from the time Rodjer was buried on the comet, and we found out that he should’ve frozen down there.” 
“What?” Laurel gasped in surprise. 
“We discovered that his temperature neglected to keep falling at the rate it started to fall.” Juniper’s voice continued quietly. “His temperature leveled off and stayed stable until we found him.” 
“We spent a good deal of time figuring it out while you all were gone.” Willow added quietly. 
“Did you discover what caused his temperature to do that?” Laurel asked with a questioning look at both of them. 
“As near as we could track it down, it seems that Rodjer’s temperature quit falling as a result of the picture he held in his mind.” Juniper paused and looked at Laurel. “You remember I had him picture something that I hoped would work on him like your biofeedback had worked for you? Well . . . Amelia says that it must have affected him enough to hold his temperature.”
“There’s data that shows he might’ve even been able to increase his temperature a bit.” Willow added. 
“That’s amazing.” Laurel stated with awe in her voice. 
“Yes.” Juniper spoke slowly, her face very serious. “Who would have thought that your biofeedback experience from years ago would help to save an AI?” Juniper leaned over and kissed Laurel on the cheek. “Anyway . . . I just wanted to say ‘thank you’. If it hadn’t been for what you learned with your biofeedback experience, I wouldn’t have Rodjer back.” 
“Well . . . I’m just very glad it worked.” Laurel replied softly. 
The three women sat silently for a while, each of them thinking about what might have been. How close they had come to losing someone they cared about. They sat and watched the stars twinkling in their sky, sipping their wine slowly. 
“When’s the party going to start?” Willow asked when the lull in the conversation began to get lengthy. 
“Soon . . . I imagine.” Laurel answered quietly. “When I left Lab, they were progressing through the updates quite rapidly. Apparently Mabel is quite an advanced AI, so the changes could be made more quickly then we first thought.” 
“Even if Mabel does have an ego that won’t quit, he sounds like he’s a very talented fellow.” Juniper commented. 
“Very.” Laurel answered. “It will be great if he decides to help us on some of our AI research. But that’s enough about Mabel . . . why don’t we start the party early? Want to go request some music, Juniper?” 
Soon music filled the night. The Holoband appearing on the fringe of the dance floor cranked up the volume and the three ladies began to dance. 
From where Rodjer, Rowan, Maynard, and Mabel approached from across the meadow, the music drifted out to them blending in with the night sounds. They paused as they got closer to watch the three women. Laurel sipped at her wine while swaying her hips to the slower beat. Juniper and Willow executed a series of intricate movements as they gyrated, spun, and hopped to the faster beat. Eventually Laurel was the first to spy the men, calling out to them to join the party. 
“You’re right . . . Mabel is gorgeous.” Willow commented to Laurel under her breath as the men approached. 
“And . . . should he decide to be a woman . . . he makes just as gorgeous a woman. You should ask Rowan.” Laurel whispered back. 
“You didn’t tell me you had two more beautiful ladies at home.” Mabel said when the men reached the dance floor. “These must be your daughters.” 
“Juniper, Willow, I’d like you to meet Mabel.” Rowan introduced them. “Mabel, I’d like you to meet our daughters, Juniper and Willow.” 
“I’m very pleased to meet you both.” Mabel murmured, bowing briefly in their direction. 
“Well . . . are you guys ready for some pizza and beer?” Laurel asked. “We’re starved.” 
“Sure.” Rowan answered, looking around at all the others gathered. “Why don’t you all stake out a table . . . and Laurel and I will go get the pizza. Maynard, can you come and help us with the beer? I think we’re going to need some additional help with that.” 
Maynard started for the Food Dispenser to put in their order for beer. Beer took fractionally longer for the Food Dispenser to make, so he needed to get that order in first. Laurel and Rowan followed Maynard at a more leisurely pace. Laurel murmured something about “missing” Rowan and they exchanged a lengthy kiss before wandering off, arms intertwined. 
Rodjer took a seat at the table and Juniper joined him. Willow followed them to the table, with Mabel in her wake. When all were settled down, the conversation turned to talk of Mabel’s Tavern. 
“Laurel says your Tavern is fascinating.” Juniper commented to Mabel. “How long have you been running a Tavern?” 
“I’ve only had the Tavern for a year or so.” Mabel answered. “But . . . so far . . . it has proved to be quite successful.”
“Well . . . it sounds like a fun place to visit.” Juniper stated. “Laurel says we’ll have to visit after the comet is moved.” 
“Of course . . . you must all come. We’ll have to have a grand celebration.” Mabel laughed. “We’ll open the whole Tavern to the comet movers and throw a hell of a party.”
Laurel and Rowan returned then. Each of them carried two pizzas balanced precariously in their hands. They set the four pizzas down and Maynard distributed the plates, a bowl of fresh sliced tomatoes, and the shakers of hot crushed red pepper and black pepper that he had carried over. As everyone was serving up slices, Maynard hurriedly returned to the Food Dispenser and brought back the beer. He set several pitchers at one end of the long table, and he and Willow spent the next few minutes dispensing frothy cold mugs of dark beer. 
“A toast.” Rowan announced when everyone, including Maynard and Willow, sat in front of a piece of pizza and a mug of beer. “A toast to the newest AI to acquire the ability to taste. May he enjoy his new found ability to taste as much as we enjoy ours . . . now . . . dig in.” 
Mabel was aware that all eyes were on him as he took his first bite. Willow had told him that to eat pizza in the Tremaine-style he must sprinkle on a good supply of black pepper and crushed red pepper and balance a tomato slice on top. Mabel could’ve taken individual bites of each ingredient to assess the effect of each separate taste that makes up the whole. Instead, he just bit into the conglomeration of ingredients. And he found that the pizza was delicious. He managed to detect and savor the individual taste sensations even as he enjoyed the whole taste combination. 
“This is great. In fact . . . it’s better than great.” Mabel stopped eating long enough to comment. “This is the best thing I’ve ever tasted.” 
“It’s the only thing you’ve ever tasted.” Was the universal response. 
And they were right. The only other tasting Mabel had done, had involved a few lab samples of basic taste tests set to regulate his adjustments. But even without comparison data, Mabel knew this meal was tasty. He wondered what other taste sensations were waiting for him, vowing to experience as much as he could as soon as he could. He found eating was very pleasurable. 
“Don’t forget the precautions we mentioned.” Rowan warned when he saw the rate at which the pizza was disappearing from Mabel’s plate. “Take it easy at first. Your system has to adjust to lots of new and different data. How did you like the beer?”
“I’ve been so busy enjoying the pizza . . . I’ve neglected to try it. Just a minute.” Mabel paused and tasted the beer. “This is wonderful. I’ll have to compare it to the beers I now serve in my Tavern. If this proves just as tasty, I’ll have to bargain with you for the formula.” Mabel grinned at Rowan. 
“Oh . . . I imagine I could be talked into giving you the formula. Maybe in exchange for free beer every time I visit the Tavern.” Rowan laughed, and then he sipped from his almost empty mug. “Maybe I’ll have to insist on driving a hard bargain and make sure that Maynard also gets a free supply when he visits. He’s grown quite fond of this beer, and he was quite helpful in developing the formula.” 
“That sounds like a deal.” Mabel replied. “Now . . . could someone please pass me another slice of pizza? A big slice, if there is one.” 
From her place at the table where she sat between Maynard and Laurel, Willow ate her pizza slowly and watched Mabel. While everyone else at the table was eating their food and enjoying it immensely, Mabel seemed to devour his. He ate his pizza elegantly and with perfect manners, but he still managed to seem to devour it. Almost like some elegant jungle beast devouring a tasty morsel. The motions were eminently civilized. But the action was primal, almost savage. She watched Mabel intently, and then suddenly realized he was gazing at her. She dropped her gaze quickly onto her plate and concentrated on eating the last of her pizza. But she could still feel Mabel’s gaze as he watched her. 
The four pizzas literally disappeared. Soon only a few forlorn crumbs were left. And then they disappeared as Laurel scooped up the olives that had managed to escape when people lifted pizza slices onto their plates. Rowan requested some music and Laurel and Rowan left the table to slow dance on the dance floor. Juniper and Rodjer soon followed, while Maynard sat at the end of the table watching and sipping yet another full mug of beer. Willow found herself at the table, staring across at Mabel. 
“Would you like to dance?” Mabel asked to put Willow out of the uncomfortable position she seemed to find herself in, trying to make conversion with a stranger. 
“Yes.” Willow smiled at Mabel. “Thank you.” 
Willow joined Mabel on the dance floor. He took her in his arms as they began to dance to the slow dance the Holoband was playing. Mabel was a very good dance partner, very smooth. Willow danced in his arms for a while, and then looked up into his face intending to make conversation. Her breath caught in her throat and no sound came out of her mouth when she opened it. Mabel was unbelievably gorgeous. He was looking down at her. Then the intensity of his gaze changed and Willow suddenly felt like a piece of pizza on his plate. 
“You dance beautifully.” Mabel complimented Willow. 
“Thank you.” Willow answered a little uneasily. “You dance very well.” 
“Why . . . thank you, fair lady.” Mabel paused in the dance to bow gallantly towards Willow. Then they resumed their slow dance. Mabel caught Willow’s look as she watched Juniper and Rodjer dancing nearby. 
“They make a fine looking couple.” Mabel commented as he watched the other couple dance. “She’s very lucky to have a bedmate of his caliber to service her.” 
“Don’t . . . don’t ever let her hear you say that.” Willow gasped a bit loudly. She immediately dropped her voice so those around them wouldn’t hear. “Even though Juniper keeps Rodjer technically updated in that area, she’s not serviced by him.” 
“Why ever not?” Mabel followed Willow’s lead and kept his questioning voice low. 
“She prefers not to be.” Willow answered simply. 
“But . . . I don’t understand.” Mabel looked questioningly at her. 
“Well . . . I can’t very well explain here.” Willow glanced around at those dancing close to them. Then she looked up at Mabel. “Would you like to go for a walk on the beach? We could talk more freely there. Besides . . . it’s hard to talk above the music, anyway.” 
“A walk on the beach would be delightful.” Mabel answered promptly. “I noticed the sounds of it as we were walking over here.” 
“OK . . . then . . . let’s go.” Willow replied, and then looked at the others dancing around them. “Just give me a minute to say good night.” 
Slight looks of surprise showed on the faces of her family as Willow told them good night and left the party with Mabel. Willow led Mabel quickly through the meadow and its surrounding trees. Before long they were standing on the stretch of grass that bordered the beach. The sounds of the surf were loud now. Moonlight shimmered off the water and made each wave look like it was sculpted out of vibrant green crystal.
“It’s beautiful.” Mabel spoke quietly. “I’ve seen pictures of beaches and read about them. But this is the first time I’ve ever actually visited one.” 
“I’m glad you like it.” Willow said as she sat down to remove her shoes. Mabel sat down on the grass beside her, following her lead. They left their shoes in a cluttered pile and started walking down the beach. 
“We’re very proud of our beach.” Willow stated, her pride showing in her voice. “We’ve made some changes though, so it’s not exactly like the Hawaiian beach we fashioned it after. Our ocean doesn’t contain salt water and it has AI inhabitants created by us.” 
“I’d very much like to see your ocean inhabitants sometime.” Mabel commented. “I’d love to try swimming, too. The accounts of the activity sound fun.” 
“You really don’t know everything . . . do you?” Willow stopped walking and stared at Mabel. “You must be knowledgeable . . . since you’re an advanced AI . . . but you don’t know everything.” 
“Actually . . . there’s a more accurate way to describe me. I know nearly everything . . . but I’ve experienced very few things.” Mabel looked down at Willow. The look on his face broadened into a grin. “But . . . I’m very good at the things I have experienced.” He paused, his look intensifying. “Most of my experience has been in two areas. The first area is as a bedmate. The second area and the only other ‘hands on’ . . .” Here he laughed . . . a deep rumbling laugh. “The only other ‘hands on’ experience I’ve had is running my Tavern.” Mabel paused again and Willow found her heart had begun pounding rather loudly. “I do both very successfully.”
“I believe you.” Willow murmured. She turned and started walking again. 
Willow led the way down into the surf, and they spent some time just walking in the small waves of water that came up to meet them. Then Mabel started the conversation again.
“You said you were going to explain to me why Juniper doesn’t use Rodjer as a bedmate.” Mabel sounded genuinely puzzled. “He’s fully equipped with the latest technology has to offer. Why wouldn’t she take advantage of that?” 
“Well . . . I don’t quite know . . . just how to explain this to you.” Willow paused and sat down in the sand. It was still warm from the sun and it felt good. 
“There are two types of meals . . . fast food and gourmet meals.” Willow paused to look at Mabel carefully. “You’ve only eaten one meal so far . . . but maybe you can still understand what I mean, if you use your imagination.”
“Fast food tastes good . . . it’s fast to get . . . and generally you eat a fast food meal fast and then go on about your business.” Willow tried to read Mabel’s face in the moonlight to see if any of this was making sense to him.
“Gourmet meals taste good, too. There are some outstanding gourmet meals that taste a lot better than any fast food meal ever could. But gourmet meals take time. They take a good deal of time to plan and prepare. And they also take a fair amount of time to eat . . . if one is to enjoy them properly. More than likely, after you eat a gourmet meal . . . you’ll sit back and relax and recall the meal you just ate over a glass of Brandy.” Willow continued her off beat explanation. 
“Well . . . Juniper chooses to eat only gourmet meals . . . where sex is concerned.” Willow gazed out at the ocean for a bit, trying to pick her words carefully. “Sex is also better with someone you know well and care about. In fact . . . I personally feel the better you know the person, the better the sex is. But . . . that’s my personal opinion.” She paused. “I think Juniper would also choose to have sex only with a free agent. In other words, she wouldn’t want Rodjer to feel like he had to be her bedmate as part of his duty . . . since she created him.” Willow paused and looked at Mabel. “She knows what she likes best, and she’s willing to wait for it.” 
“That sounds complicated.” Mabel commented. He had been listening quietly to Willow while sifting handfuls of sand through his fingers. “When, or even if, Juniper manages to get all that sorted out . . . she might have a nice concept. But she misses a lot of fun.” 
“Yes . . . you’re probably right. But I guess Juniper feels some things are worth the wait.” Willow answered quietly. 
“What about you?” Mabel stopped sifting, turning to watch Willow closely. “Do you view sex like your sister does?” 
“Well . . . before I answer that question . . . how do you view sex?” Willow stalled for time. Besides she was interested to find out more about him. 
“Me?” Mabel grinned down at her. “Pretty lady . . . I’m very easy to please. I like a good fast food meal . . . even a fast food meal with a stranger.” His eyes seemed to light at the idea. “Also . . . on occasion . . . I’ve been known to plan and prepare and partake of a scrumptious gourmet meal with a repeat customer. I think basically . . . when you come down to it . . . I just like to eat whatever’s on my plate.” 
“Am I on your menu . . . for tonight?” Willow was surprised to hear herself say the words. 
“Oh . . . yes.” Mabel paused and his eyes did their slow dance down her body. “The only thing left to determine . . . is whether you skitter off my plate before I get a chance to taste my meal.”
“Oh . . .” Willow murmured very low, looking away. She didn’t want to look at him. In fact, she didn’t think she could. Instead, she sat and watched the moon and the waves and carefully avoided looking at the man to her left. She knew what she wanted, but she was almost too afraid to get it. 
Mabel was dangerous for her. She’d have to be very careful or she’d get very hurt. She remembered how Mabel had looked as he ate his pizza. How civilized a being was this creature beside her? He was very elegant and very good looking, but beneath the polished surface was a very advanced AI that hadn’t been in existence very long. 
Willow wasn’t surprised to find herself fascinated by him. She had always had a very special attraction for animals. She thrived on the challenge involved in learning about them. Mabel was very much like an animal. His actions were motivated either by the instinct for survival or pleasure. Morals weren’t part of his experience yet. Willow was deeply attracted to the opportunity to learn more about Mabel. But the learning would involve observing Mabel as he did what he does best. And there in lay the danger for her. 
“Well . . . you still haven’t answered my question.” Mabel prodded Willow out of her continued silence. “What is your view of sex?” 
“Oh . . . it depends.” Willow got up, and brushed off the seat of her shorts and looked down at Mabel. She realized again as the moonlight lit his face, that he had very ancient eyes. There were moments, when his eyes looked a million years old. She must be careful, very careful, not to expect more of him than it was in his nature to give. 
“Usually . . .” Willow found herself grinning into Mabel’s eyes. “I just start eating and the meal turns into whatever is right.” 
“That sounds unpredictable and fun.” Mabel laughed, his eyes twinkling in the moonlight. 
“Well . . . shall we keep walking down the beach?” Willow paused, watching his expression carefully. “Or . . . would you like me to service you?” 
“What did you say?” Mabel mumbled, after a heartbeat of silence. 
“You heard me. Do you want to keep walking down the beach? Or . . . would you like me to service you?” Willow continued to watch Mabel’s expression carefully, but she had seen enough to resume breathing. Unconsciously she had been holding her breath.
Mabel still remained silent, gazing at Willow. 
“You haven’t answered yet.” Willow prompted him. “Your pause is no doubt due to a need to contemplate my question. It wouldn’t be caused by your belief that I wouldn’t be any good . . . would it?” 
“Perish the thought.” Mabel seemed surprised at himself. “I’ll take my chances gladly in that respect.” 
Mabel stood up and bowed formally, and extended his hand. 
“I accept with honor, fair lady. I accept your generous offer of servicing my humble self.” Mabel said gallantly.
Willow shook his hand, a small smile just barely lighting her eyes. She hoped her face was in shadow, it wouldn’t do to assume victory too soon. 
“OK . . . then . . . let’s go to my dome.” Willow turned and started to walk determinedly back the way they had come. 
The flashes of insight she had gotten about him were just too fascinating. She had to know him better. She just sincerely hoped she knew what she was doing. 

* * *

The dome Willow occupied was located on the outskirts of the meadow. She announced her presence and the door opened. Keeko ran smack into her and rubbed against her legs. Willow stooped to pick up the baby dinosaur. 
“How are you doing, Keeko?” Willow asked softly as she entered the dome with Mabel following close behind her. 
“That looks like a real dinosaur.” Mabel commented as he watched the bouncing flash of fur disappear into a corner. 
“I’ve always been a big fan of dinosaurs. So I made some genetic engineering changes to cat DNA.” Willow stated proudly as the door closed behind them. “Keeko is the result.” 
“That’s amazing.” Mabel complimented her. 
“Have a seat on the bed.” Willow gestured helpfully in the general direction where her bed was located. “It’s the only piece of furniture in here. I’ll get some light in just a second.” 
Willow returned a moment later with a candle. Its soft flickering glow bounced off the walls as she sat down facing Mabel. She cleared some space and carefully put the candle on the small cluttered table by the bed. In contrast to the table, her bed was neat as a pin. The gigantic water bed seemed to take up at least a third of the room. 
“Now . . .” Willow leaned back on the neatly arranged pillows and watched Mabel carefully. “How can I be of service to you?” 
“It’s a bit different to have the tables switched. I usually ask that question.” Mabel answered, watching Willow as her blonde hair rippled when she moved. “I want you to show me what ‘gourmet meal’ sex would be like with you caring about me a lot.” 
“Let me make sure I understand your request.” Willow spoke carefully. “You want me to service you as we already agreed, by showing you what ‘gourmet meal’ sex would be like with me caring about you a lot. Is that correct?” 
“Yes.” Mabel watched her carefully, a grin spreading across his face. “That’s exactly what I want.” 
“Well . . . that’s easy enough.” Willow said, a smile finding its way into her eyes. “First . . . I spend lots of time with you. I’d say . . . to do the job properly . . . I’d probably need . . . oh . . . months at least. Then when I come to really care about you, I’ll give you that gourmet meal.” She got up and went to the Food Dispenser. 
“As a first step towards spending time together . . . I thought we could share a hot fudge sundae. You’ll have the opportunity to learn that I love hot fudge sundaes. And . . . we’ll both find out whether you like hot fudge sundaes or not. It’ll be one giant step towards getting to know each other . . . the first step towards starting to care.” 
Willow returned to the bed with a giant bowl of hot fudge sundae. Handing Mabel one of the long handled spoons, she sat down cross-legged on the bed and started to dip her spoon into the luscious ice cream. 
“This is it?” Mabel was astonished. “We’re going to sit on your bed and eat a giant hot fudge sundae.” 
“Of course.” Willow smiled at him. “Don’t worry. I’m sure you’ll like it. Here.” She said, dipping her spoon into the bowl for a heaping spoonful. 
She carefully moved the spoonful of ice cream until she held it suspended in the air in front of Mabel’s mouth. He looked at the spoonful of ice cream, looked at her, and then looked back at the ice cream and opened his mouth. 
“There. Isn’t that good?” Willow watched him swallow the spoonful. “We’re going to eat our sundae and get to know each other.” Then she paused and sat back, grinning at Mabel. “Oh . . . you didn’t think I’d cheat you out of a good job, did you? Did you think that I’d just pretend to care about you . . . while I showed you ‘gourmet meal’ sex?” She paused. “I take my offer of service very seriously . . . as I’m sure you do when you’re asked to service someone. You can be assured I’ll do a good job. When I give you that gourmet meal, I’ll genuinely care about you.” The smile faded from Willow’s face and her voice grew serious. “I’d never fake that. How could I take pride in delivering my part of our bargain . . . if I didn’t give you exactly what you asked for?” She smiled warmly into his eyes. 
“I’ve just been had.” Mabel stated in a low voice. He was very surprised at himself. He hadn’t even seen it coming. 
This beautiful lady who was sitting in front of him, grinning at him, had out maneuvered him. As far as he could recollect this had never happened to him before, and he could recollect a long time past. No one out maneuvered him when it came to making bargains. And yet, this wisp of a human lady had done it. He had agreed to be serviced by her. He had made his request. He couldn’t even argue with her logic. She was going to give him what he wanted. The only catch was it would take months to do it properly. 
He had definitely been had. He knew it. And he knew she knew it. She was enjoying herself far too much. 
“Now . . . why don’t you sit back and eat some of our sundae and tell me all about yourself.” Willow spooned a giant scoop of whip cream smothered ice cream into her mouth. “Just a second, though.” She jumped up, and began to remove her shorts. “Let me get comfy.” And she was stating the truth. She could sit more comfortably without her shorts on. “That’s much better.” Willow stated as she sat back down again. “You sure . . . you don’t want to get comfy, too? It’s going to be a long night.” 
Mabel stared at her. Willow’s slim legs ended in the prettiest pair of purple lace panties he had ever seen. And he had seen quite a few. 
“Willow . . . ah . . .” Mabel paused. He had a strange expression on his face and Willow could almost hear his brain working. “Willow . . . ah . . . could I service you tonight?” Mabel asked in an unusually humble voice. 
“I thought you’d never ask.” Willow grinned at him. “Yes. I’d like that very much.” She leaned over and kissed Mabel. “I don’t think it will in any way effect the bargain I’ve already made with you. Don’t you agree? Our first bargain can still remain in effect, while I make another bargain with you to service me.” 
“I can’t think of one reason why we can’t have as many bargains as we want with each other.” Mabel agreed helpfully. 
“Good.” Willow paused, tilting her head to the side as she gazed at him. “Now . . . what do I want?” She looked into his gorgeous eyes glowing in the candle light. 
“I think I’ll just ask for a fast food meal or a gourmet meal . . . whichever it turns out to be.” She grinned at Mabel, her eyes twinkling with mischief. “And . . . I want it with an almost total stranger.” 
“Thank God . . .” Mabel grinned back at Willow as he began to lift her t-shirt over her head. “Thank God . . . your request I can deliver right now and still insure you get everything you want.” He began to kiss the delicate parts of her neck, where her blonde cascade of hair began its long journey to touch the top of her silk panties. “And . . . then some.” He added under his breath. But Willow’s heart was beating so loudly, she didn’t hear. 

* * *

Sunlight peeped over the trees and into Willow’s window the next morning. As the warm rays fell across her face, Willow awoke to find Mabel watching her. He was leaning up against the pillows under the window, just watching her. 
“Hi.” Willow grinned at him, brushing a long strand of her hair out of her eyes. 
“I must compliment you on your delivery of your services last night.” Her grin broadened. “You gave me a great gourmet meal with an almost total stranger. So perfect and so realistic . . . especially the almost stranger part.” Willow reached up and kissed Mabel. “And . . . I’m now even further along in my quest to service you. I know you so much better already. Yes . . . I’d say I’m well on my way to eventually knowing you well.”  
“Are you always this cheery in the morning?” Mabel asked gazing appreciatively as the sunlight lit up the parts of her that had been in shadow. 
“Well . . . I must admit I am a bit more cheery today.” Willow grinned at him as she jumped up and walked towards the Food Dispenser. “And . . . for the life of me . . . I can’t seem to figure out why. I’m sure that being soundly fucked by a very advanced and very talented AI bedmate . . . had nothing to do with it.” 
“You’re sure about that?” Mabel asked, surprised that he found talking to this woman so interesting. But after last night, his impression of her had swiftly grown to fascination. Partly due to the way she had out maneuvered him in the bargain, and partly because of what had transpired later. 
“Of course . . . I’m sure.” Willow stomped back to face Mabel, her fists clenched on her slim hips. “Are you saying . . . I don’t know a good fuck when I get it? How dare you doubt me? I know what you did last night was fantastic . . . and outrageous . . . and just plain wonderful. If you doubt your talents . . . you’ll just have to take my word for it.” 
Mabel threw back his head and laughed. It was the kind of laugh that bubbles up and rolls out of somewhere deep inside a person. Willow found it a very sexy sound. She watched him, very proud of herself. She had pulled it off. She knew she had managed to interest him in a way no one ever had before. And as a bonus, she had also managed to find a way to protect herself. There was no way he could get up this morning and disappear out of her life. She had a bargain with him. 
Like lightening, Mabel reached out a hand and instantly pulled Willow back into bed with him. 
“You’re priceless.” He said, gazing into her carefully innocent face. “You know that’s not what I meant.” 
“Oh . . . do I?” Willow laughed up into his face. “Maybe . . . you should offer to service me again and we can see if I get noticeably more cheery. You know . . . logically and scientifically prove beyond a shadow of a doubt . . . the reason why I’m so cheery this morning.”
Mabel didn’t answer. He just planted a good, healthy, resounding, heartfelt, deep, probing kiss smack on her laughing mouth. When he finished, he just leaned back and repeated what he had said before. 
“You’re priceless.” He grinned at her. “I’m going to enjoy eventually getting my request. I just have to endure through all the long months until you do that servicing that you’re going to do.” 
Willow grinned back at him. 
“Well . . . if it gets too tough . . . you can always offer to service me again. You’re very good at your job . . . you know. Damn good.” 
“That’s a good idea. If I’m lucky . . . each time I service you . . . you’ll get to know me better and I’ll be that much closer to the time you service me.” He laughed. “And . . . I end up helping you to service me.” 
“There you go.” Willow reached to pull his fine dark head closer, kissing his cheek. “Now . . . you’ve got the proper attitude about all this. Just sit back and make the best of it.”
“Well . . . I thought I’d made the best of it . . . but . . . we could try again.” Mabel said scooping her up into his arms. 
“Food. Food. I must have food first.” Willow squirmed away from him. “What would you like for your second meal ever? Ah . . . make that your third meal ever?” She corrected herself, grinning back at him over her shoulder.
“I’m willing to try anything you want.” Mabel laughed. “Then . . . when we’re done . . . you can choose what we request from the Food Dispenser.” 
Willow laughed with him as she crawled back into bed. He was beginning to get a sense of humor. Surely, a good sense of humor was the first step towards becoming a civilized man. 

## Chapter 5 — CHANGES




Once again Lab was as crowded as it ever got. During the night, Amelia and Maynard had worked out the appearance and structural changes that would be needed to make him a man. After Maynard had made the final decisions, Amelia had started the nanotechnology machines constructing the various pieces of hardware that would be needed to transform him. 
Mabel had spent the morning capturing a spare moment here and there with Maynard, going over all the bedmate updates he might want. Long ago Amelia had also started to create the necessary equipment for those changes as well. 
Now as the afternoon hours dwindled away, everyone gathered in Lab to watch and wait as Maynard was transformed. 
Maynard lay outstretched on one of the worktables with Rowan, Mabel, and Rodjer bent over him. His inner and outer construction parts and assemblies were scattered about in a surprising array of what looked like bits and pieces barely strung together by spaghetti. His central core was hooked directly to Amelia, while she updated his software very rapidly. It all made a bit of a strange scene. For Maynard remained aware of not only Amelia’s updates, but the hardware updates being carried out by those gathered around the table. 
Juniper, Willow, and Laurel roamed the room. Alternately they sat on the couch and commented to each other about the differences that would result with Maynard being a man or they stood and leaned over Maynard to talk to him and watch and comment on the changes taking place or they sat again and talked and snacked while they continued to wait. 
Gradually Maynard began to take on the shape of a man, and a pile of furry teddy bear parts began to grow on the floor in a corner of Lab. When Mabel began to attach the special hardware that had been built by the nanotechnology machines to enable Maynard to become a bedmate, everyone became unusually interested. 
Gathering around the table, Juniper and Willow and Laurel had to be consciously aware so they didn’t get in the way. The intricate nature of the hardware which fashioned Maynard in this area was fascinating. Rodjer noticed that Mabel was including in Maynard some updates that he himself would like to acquire. Mabel was quite generous to offer to update him later, if Rowan supplied the raw materials for the nanotechnology machines. And since Rowan was always glad to update his AIs in any way possible, he readily agreed. 
“Well . . . that about does it.” Rowan commented as he finished attaching a portion of Maynard’s hardware. “Now . . . we just have to determine if there are any bugs to be worked out.” 
Maynard sat up and climbed off the table. Like royalty, a path opened up for him through the crowd and he walked to the center of Lab. Then with looks of awe on their faces, his flock of loyal subjects gathered around. 
“How do I look?” Maynard asked and allowed himself a slow pirouette in front of his speechless audience. 
Maynard had chosen for himself a swarthy complexion of burnished copper. His black curly hair fell to his shoulders and his eyes were brilliant. Instead of the blue of deep ocean water as Juniper had chosen for Rodjer, Maynard had chosen to make his eyes the light green color of tropical shore line water. The combination was breathtaking. Maynard grinned at everyone as he finished his slow circle.
“A bit different . . . wouldn’t you say?” Maynard commented about himself. 
“God . . . you’re so handsome.” Laurel came up to him and had to stand on tiptoe to kiss his copper cheek. “You have such a gorgeous body.” 
“All part of the bedmate package.” Mabel announced from the side lines. “He’s now fully stocked and ready for action.” 
“Well . . . I could still use some practice.” Maynard glanced over at Mabel. “You said you’d teach me some things you’ve learned . . . things that can’t be put into software.” 
“Yes. I did promise that, didn’t I?” Mabel answered. “And I shall deliver. But . . . the job entails a change for me, doesn’t it?”
“Yes. I did want to learn how to be a proper bedmate for women.” Maynard admitted. 
“Well . . . you choose the place . . . and I’ll meet you there completely transformed into a woman.” Mabel smiled at Maynard.
“Amelia.” Maynard spoke. “Could I use the Beach Shack tonight?” 
“Yes.” Amelia answered. “It’s unspoken for, so you may use it if you’d like.”
“Thank you.” Maynard replied. “I’ll meet you at the Beach Shack tonight, Mabel.” 
“That sounds like a plan.” Mabel answered. “Why don’t you walk around a bit and check to make sure everything is in working order.” 
“OK.” Maynard glanced around at all those trying to adjust to his new appearance. “I want to thank each of you for your support in accepting my change and your help in enabling me to achieve it.” He smiled and his look fell on the discarded pile of furry bits in the corner. “I guess I’ll clean up around here and then go take a walk. Try out my new legs. There’s definitely a difference viewing things from this height.” Maynard paused and looked down at himself. “I guess I could use some clothes as well.”
He accepted a pair of shorts and t-shirt that Rowan had thoughtfully brought for him. After he dressed, Maynard started to go and collect what remained of his old self.
“That’s all right.” Laurel said, stopping Maynard as he made his way towards the pile of discarded parts. “You go ahead and try out those legs of yours. I’d like to clean up the bits and pieces of the old Maynard. It will be my last kindness to the Maynard you used to be.” Laurel went over and started to pick up a piece of fur which included the cream colored section that used to reside over Maynard’s stomach. “Would you mind if I made a stuffed teddy bear out of these bits of fur? I just hate to throw them all in the Recycle Slot.” 
“Laurel.” Maynard came over and squatted down beside her. “You can do whatever you want with these pieces.” He reached out and turned her face, so she was forced to look into his eyes. “I know my change is hardest on you.” Maynard leaned down and kissed Laurel’s forehead gently. “I just want you to know . . . that it’s still the old me in here. Under all this fancy decoration, the same Maynard exists that you so carefully created.” He held out his newly created hand in front of him. “I just changed a bit and added on some here and there. But . . . I’m still here . . . and I care about you deeply.” His brilliant green eyes gazed intently into hers. “It’s going to take me a while to get used to the changes, too. So . . . you’re not alone in that regard, either.” 
“You’re so sweet.” Laurel kissed him lightly. Maynard understood some of what she was going through. Maybe someday he would realize just how much his change affected her. Instead of a proper-acting little furry teddy bear that happened to be very smart and very talented, she now would be spending time with a very smart and very talented gorgeous man who was built to be a bedmate. Whose eyes, when they sparkled with Maynard-type kindness and consideration, made her heart melt. 
“You go ahead and try out those legs. I’m just going to gather up a few pieces, and then I’ll recycle the rest.” Laurel assured him. 
“I’ll see you in the morning, then.” Maynard got up to leave. “I’m likely to be busy all night.” 
Maynard turned to leave. Laurel watched him go as in her hand she stroked a piece of fur that used to be him. A tear found its way out of her eye and begun its journey down her cheek. Rowan reached out and caught it with his finger, wiping the tear away.
“It’s going to be all right.” Rowan put an arm tenderly around Laurel. “After the others go, I’ll help you sort through the pieces.” 
“Thanks.” Laurel leaned her head on his shoulder. “I think this is going to be harder than I thought.” 
They stood together then, and talked a bit with the others. About how good Maynard looked and how changed he was. Then the others left, and Rowan and Laurel were alone in Lab. 
“Amelia could have her nanotechnology machines create a darling stuffed teddy bear for you. She could make it look just like the old Maynard.” Rowan commented helpfully. 
“I know. But she would have to break down all these bits and pieces, and then build the bear out of the raw materials.” Laurel reached over and picked up a furry piece. “I want to save some of this very fur. Just a few of the darker bits and one or two of the cream colored pieces, so I can give my stuffed teddy bear a cream colored stomach. Then I’ll have Amelia make up some polyester stuffing and thread to match. I’ll just sew a little teddy bear by hand.” She looked up into Rowan’s concerned eyes. “You know me . . . sometimes I just like to do things myself. The end result is much more valuable to me.” 
“OK. What can I do to help?” Rowan kissed her cheek. Then he bent to help Laurel sort through all that remained of the old Maynard as she had created him. 
They spent the next half hour or so sorting carefully to find the best and prettiest pieces of fur. After stacking them on the edge of the couch, Laurel and Rowan made numerous trips to the Recycle Slot to dispose of what was left. They quietly and carefully put away all the tools and bits and pieces that had been brought out and used in the assembling of the new Maynard. When everything was neat and clean again, Laurel gathered up her pile of fur. 
“Amelia.” Laurel asked. “Could you make me up a pound or two of polyester stuffing and some thread to match Maynard’s fur? I’ll need thread to match both the light and darker bits of fur.” 
“Laurel.” Amelia answered. “I already took the liberty of making those items, since you mentioned them earlier. They’re waiting in the Lab Dispenser.” 
“Why, thank you Amelia.” Laurel murmured. “That was a very thoughtful thing for you to do.” 
“You’re very welcome.” Amelia answered. 
Rowan collected the stuffing and thread, found a needle, and got a pair of scissors from a drawer. Then he followed Laurel out into the early evening light. They walked slowly back to their dome. They talked almost non-stop about this and that that they had done with the old Maynard. Then they spent some time wondering what changes the new Maynard would effect in the old. They knew that he was still the old Maynard underneath, but they also knew that any experience changes a person. And Maynard’s changes were sure to change him as well. 
Inside their dome, Rowan put the sewing supplies down and got them each a hot mug of coffee. He brought the coffee back to the table where Laurel was already sketching patterns on pieces of paper. He returned to the Food Dispenser and brought back a plate of deli sandwiches and chips. Together they munched down their dinner while Laurel scribbled and planned on paper. By the time they had finished eating, Laurel had a plan in mind and the next two hours were taken up with cutting, sewing, and stuffing.
At the end of the evening, Laurel held in her lap a medium-sized stuffed bear, complete with a cream colored furry stomach. Maybe it wasn’t a perfect teddy bear, but it was very nice and cute in its own way. All it needed was some eyes. Laurel reached into the pocket of her shorts and pulled out two pieces of tiny hardware bits that had once been part of Maynard’s innards. 
Before she could begin to sew them on the bear as makeshift eyes, Rowan reached out his hand towards her and opened his fist slowly. Inside his hand lay two perfect bear eyes, just the color that Maynard’s eyes used to be. 
“They’re perfect.” Laurel spoke in a whisper. “Where’d you get them?” 
“While you were busy sewing and stuffing, I had Amelia make them.” Rowan answered with a small smile. “I thought you might like them.” 
With a grateful glance in Rowan’s direction, Laurel carefully sewed the tiny eyes in place. The tiny eyes were perfect eyes, catching the light and reflecting it brilliantly. She smiled at Rowan and held out the bear for his inspection. 
“Very nice.” Rowan said with a smile. “He’s a very handsome bear. He just lacks one final touch.” 
And with that said, Rowan brought out a piece of bright jade green ribbon from his pocket. 
“I asked Amelia to make this, too, just in case you could use it.” He smiled at Laurel.
“When did you do all this?” Laurel asked. “I don’t remember you requesting anything from Amelia.” 
“You were so engrossed in the sewing stage.” Rowan answered. “I guess you just didn’t hear. Besides . . . I whispered.” 
Laurel carefully tied the ribbon around the little bear’s neck. The color perfectly matched the color of the little bear’s eyes. 
“Thanks. It’s just perfect.” Laurel smiled at Rowan. “He makes a nice bear . . . doesn’t he?”
“Yes.” Rowan steered her towards their bed. “A very nice bear. Now . . . let’s go to bed. I think you’ve had a very hard day today.” 
Laurel and Rowan stripped quickly and quietly and climbed into bed. Snuggled down spoon-style, Laurel went to sleep with Rowan’s arms holding her and her arms clutching her new teddy bear. 

* * *

Night had fallen swiftly. Maynard got up from where he had been sitting in the sand watching the horizon’s shifting colors. So far his body had performed well. His legs worked. His arms worked. His eyes worked. Now he slowly made his way to the Beach Shack to see if the rest of him worked as well. 
Maynard was a little hesitant about the next step in his process of change. It was important to him to understand firsthand what being a bedmate was like. He enjoyed gaining new information of any kind. This was one reason why he enjoyed the research project that he and Amelia had been working on for some time now. There were many intricate variations and constant new information when they observed and researched AI and human interactions. He had observed that a great deal of energy and interest was used up by humans in a preoccupation with sex. And having met Mabel, he realized that sex could take up a large amount of an AI’s energy as well. So in an effort to learn and experience more, he had desired the changes that had taken place. 
While he knew much that was written about sex, the actual experience of sex was a bit foreboding to him. He just hoped with Mabel’s help, he could get good enough at it to be able to get some reliable data to further his research. He had to admit that having a sense of taste and experiencing eating had helped him greatly to get a handle on understanding humans. So he hoped that sex would be an eye opener as well. 
When Maynard approached the Beach Shack from the beach, he found the outside light on. As he entered, he could make out a form lounging on the bed under the window. He spoke and the lights in the room brightened. He could see that the form was Mabel. The woman he had met at the Tavern. She was very beautiful. 
“Hello, Maynard.” Mabel acknowledged his presence. “How was your walk? Is everything in working order?” 
“Yes.” Maynard moved to sit on the bed beside her. “I’ve detected nothing so far, that would require adjustments.” 
Maynard watched Mabel in the semi-darkness and found himself a bit nervous. He had been nervous before and recognized the signs. 
“Would you mind if I have a snack?” Maynard asked. “I’d like to have some dip and chips, if you don’t mind.” 
“That sounds good.” Mabel smiled at him. “Would you mind getting some for me, too? I realize how much you seem to enjoy dip and chips, and I want to try them and see for myself.” 
Maynard went to the Food Dispenser and returned a moment later with two bowls of nacho cheese dip and a giant bowl of tortilla chips. He set them down on the table by the bed and sat down again beside Mabel.
“We could’ve shared a big bowl of dip together.” Mabel commented as she picked up a chip and munched it slowly. 
“Oh . . . well . . . I wasn’t sure you would want that.” Maynard smiled hesitantly. “We can share a small one and just use the other one when that one runs out.” 
“That’s a great idea.” Mabel reached over and put the big bowl of chips on the bed between them and Maynard moved a bowl of dip to set beside it. 
“I just love cheese.” Maynard commented as he dipped a large chip quite liberally into the dip. 
“Yes . . . I’ve noticed that.” Mabel dipped a chip and munched a bit of it. “This really is quite good. No wonder you like it so much.” 
“You like it, then?” Maynard asked, watching as she licked a bit of dip from the tip of her index finger. 
“Yes, it’s quite tasty.” Mabel answered, catching his look and reaching out her finger to dip it delicately in the bowl of dip. 
“Might I ask?” Maynard paused to take another bite. “How are we going to proceed after our snack is over? I must admit that I’m a little uncertain as to what to do.” 
“Well . . . luckily for you . . . you have me to help you.” Mabel smiled at him with eyes that contained a look Maynard had not noticed Mabel having before . . . sort of an intense look, making her eyes shine a bit more brightly. “I think that we’ll stop our snack of tortilla chips and dip when you empty this first bowl of dip.” A slow grin began to grow on Mabel’s face. “Then maybe we could shift to a different type of snacking. We can still put that second bowl of nacho cheese dip to good use . . . don’t worry.” She reassured Maynard. “I think by using it in different and very innovative ways, we can get you over any feeling of hesitancy. After that, I think your natural reactions will take over.” She leaned over and kissed Maynard’s cheek. Then she allowed her tongue to find a bit of stray cheese dip on his mouth and gently lick it away. “I’m sure that once your natural reactions start, you’ll do fine. I’ll point out items of information here and there which will help you and you’ll be a pro in no time.” 
Maynard finished scooping up the last of the dip and moved the empty bowl of dip and the big bowl of chips to the table. 
“After all . . . we have all night.” Mabel said a slight smile evident on her face. “And . . . since we’re both very advanced AIs . . . we should be able to accomplish a great deal by morning.” 
“Well . . . I certainly appreciate your help in giving me additional instruction.” Maynard said as Mabel reached for the second bowl of dip and brought it back to the bed. 
“Not nearly as much . . . as you will in the morning.” Mabel murmured under her breath as she leaned towards him. 

* * *

When dawn began to lighten the sky above their ocean and streaks of delicate pink began to work their way across the sky, at least two persons were awake to see it. They were wake, but not altogether rested. 
Maynard was lying against the pillows at the head of the bed and Mabel was nestled snugly in his arms. They were resting once again. Maynard had long ago lost count of the number, but this last time had been very vigorous. Compared to human standards, an AI never gets tired. But when two AIs get going at it, they eventually will begin to drain their energy resources. 
“So?” Maynard said as he gently stroked Mabel’s luscious dark hair where it fell across her temple. “What’s left for me to learn?” 
“Well . . . I’d say you’ve learned just about everything I can teach you.” Mabel said, a slow smile spreading across her face. “I’d say from now on . . . you just proceed at your own pace. As new and innovative ideas present themselves to you, you can add them to your repertoire.” 
Mabel rolled over and propelled herself slowly up Maynard’s body until she was lying between his legs with her chin resting on his chest. 
“But you must promise me . . . if you discover any new techniques or ideas for updates, let me know. It’s kind of a graduation requirement from my school of sexual graces. Everyone agrees to share new information they discover. That way we all end up improving from the innovations of us all.” Mabel reached up to kiss his cheek. “And . . . you have just received the entire sexual knowledge and experience of a great many equally devoted fans of the sport. So . . . we would appreciate you keeping us informed of any ideas you may eventually come up with.” 
“That sounds like an equitable arrangement.” Maynard said as he kissed her nose. “I’ll definitely keep you informed of my ideas as long as I can keep names and dates confidential.” 
“Oh, of course, that goes without saying.” Mabel laughed. “That’s a rule of our trade that is centuries old.” 
“Now . . . speaking of coming up with something . . .” Maynard looked pointedly down at her. 
“Oh . . . no . . . you don’t.” Mabel sprang out of bed. “As much as I like it, one has to call it quits sooner or later. With humans, it’s easier on them. They just get too exhausted to do it any longer. And while we can get tired, which I might add is a fact known only among AIs, it takes us longer and we recover faster. Also, no matter how tired we get, we’re still able to perform.” Mabel smiled down at Maynard where he still lay on the bed. “Now . . . you have the biggest lesson of all to learn . . . when to and when not to. Some AI’s almost totally break down when they first realize what sex is like. They just do it constantly in one form or another. The rest of us eventually learn that if you do something all the time there’s no time to do anything else. And no matter how pleasurable something is, if you do it constantly it loses its special qualities.” 
“So . . . do a favor for me.” Mabel walked over to Maynard and kissed him gently and ruffled his hair. “So I can be proud I taught you . . . for I’ve taught the best. Pick the occasions carefully. It’s usually a good idea to think about the consequences first. It’s amazing how a simple thing like sex can complicate everything. And above all, always make sure the customer leaves happy. That way you ensure repeat business. And even if you’re not a working bedmate and you choose to develop a personal relationship with someone, repeat business is still your ticket to more enjoyment.”
“Now . . . I’m off to meet Willow on the beach. She promised to take me swimming and to see the AI inhabitants they made for their ocean.” Mabel grinned down at Maynard where he still leaned against the pillows in bed. 
“It was a pleasure to teach you last night. You learn very quickly . . . even for an AI.” Her smile deepened. “You may have even given me a new idea or two. So . . . thanks and good luck and keep up the good work.” Mabel leaned down and gave Maynard one more very lengthy kiss and then she vanished out the door. 
Maynard watched Mabel through the window as she walked towards the beach, her long dark hair blowing in the breeze. She was a very talented AI. While he had known that simple fact was evidenced by the hardware and software she had helped him to implement into his system, he knew it even more now. Not only was she very talented, but very complex as well. He had the feeling that she had many areas of herself that even she had not yet explored. 
With a sigh, he got to his feet. Going over to the Food Dispenser he requested a large mushroom omelet with double cheese. Taking the plate out to the little deck outside the door of the Beach Shack, he sat and ate in the sun. It took him a moment to realize that he was no longer a teddy bear and that he probably should have clothes on. But no one was around and the sun did feel very good on his furless skin.
He ate and contemplated the night that had just passed. The activities of the night had raised almost as many questions as they had answered. He now knew a great deal more about sex. The most important new fact he knew about sex, was the fact that he enjoyed it immensely. Sex also created a problem that his mind was trying desperately to solve. This morning he seemed to find himself a very talented, very smart AI who was now an accomplished bedmate but who lacked a partner. 
Mabel would be returning to her Tavern. And he could hardly be a bedmate to one of the human females. If they had wanted a bedmate, they would’ve created one long ago. He searched his mind as he ate his breakfast. 
He didn’t want to leave this ship. He enjoyed working with the people on board and was challenged by the projects in which he participated. He also very much enjoyed the respect and caring they gave him. He would miss them terribly, if he were to leave. But he also knew that he couldn’t realistically stay and not participate in sex with someone. No more than he could choose not to eat cheese. 
Maynard smiled to himself at the comparison. Actually, he could probably go without cheese easier than he could go without sex. One just involved the sense of taste, while the other involved his whole being. This need for sex was one outcome of his change he hadn’t envisioned ahead of time. He had hoped to end up with information to better understand humans and AIs. But what he had ended up with was that, plus the added bonus or problem of needing a sex partner. 
He was just returning to the Recycle Slot to dump his trash, when an idea hit him. And a smile spread across his face until he was laughing out loud. It was a strange thing for an AI to experience, the sound of himself laughing alone. He couldn’t understand, now that he had thought of it, why he hadn’t thought of it before. 
Quickly Maynard dressed in his clothes from the night before and began to straighten the Beach Shack. He stripped the bed and put clean sheets on, putting the dirty ones outside in the Laundry Slot. He gathered up all the trash and recycled the lot. Then he stood back and surveyed the room. So much had happened here, and yet not a sign of it remained anywhere. Amazing. After assuring himself that he was leaving the Beach Shack the way he had found it, he left closing the door silently behind him. 

* * *

Silently, so as not to disturb anyone who might be inside the Beach Shack, Willow left her clothes by the showers outside the Beach Shack. Early morning light filtered through the palm trees that lined the beach. The ocean was calm and beautiful in the first light as Willow walked slowly down the beach. 
“Oh . . . God.” Willow stopped short and breathed out in surprise. 
Down the beach a bit, sat a woman. The woman was sitting on the sand, looking out at the ocean and slowly sifting handfuls of sand through her fingers. Even at this distance, Willow could tell that the woman was no one she had seen before. So, by reasonable deductions, Willow realized that the woman must be Mabel. Willow had known that sooner or later this moment would arrive. It was inevitable. With Mabel able to change shape at will, it was only a matter of time until Willow saw Mabel as a woman. Willow had just hoped that it would happen later. 
Willow began walking again. She slowed her steps, studying Mabel’s appearance as she approached. The woman was beautiful. She had the same dark luscious hair that Mabel had as a man, but now the hair was very long. The woman turned to look at Willow as she approached, and Willow saw that the woman shared a resemblance to Mabel’s dark good looks as well. The moment of truth had come. 
“Hello.” Willow said with a small smile. 
“Hello, Willow.” Mabel spoke, watching Willow carefully. Mabel was curious as to how Willow would react. 
“It’s a gorgeous morning, isn’t it?” Willow sat down in the sand by Mabel.
“Yes.” Mabel smiled at her. “Your ocean looks particularly inviting in this early morning light.” 
Willow watched as the woman beside her sifted sand through her hands, the same way she had seen Mabel sifting the sand the other night, both motions mirroring each other. It was like the Mabel she had known was a chameleon, taking on the equivalent of another color when the occasion called for it. Able to fit himself into the surrounding environment in whichever persona best suited him. Willow smiled to herself as she watched the woman beside her. So different from the Mabel she knew, yet still so much the same. 
“I feel a bit strange, seeing you for the first time like this.” Willow caught the woman’s dark eyes and smiled. “Almost like . . . I should introduce myself.” Willow laughed a small laugh. “But . . . that would be silly. We’ve already met.” 
“Yes.” Mabel smiled a delicate smile in Willow’s direction. “We’ve met.” 
“Well . . . then. I’d just like to say that you’re extremely gorgeous as a woman, too.” Willow smiled slightly. “You seem to have extraordinary good taste in appearance, either way.” 
“Thank you.” Mabel acknowledged her compliment. “I must say that you’re taking the shock of my appearance rather well.” 
“Yes. I am.” Willow replied slowly. Her reaction had surprised even herself. It would seem that she had unconsciously decided to accept Mabel, to accept him or her or it with all its unique qualities. 
While a part of Willow was a bit surprised at her acceptance, another part was not. After all, what choice did she have, if she wanted to learn more about him? Mabel’s ability to change his form was an important fact of his existence. His ability wasn’t unlike the ability of many animals. Animals that changed their color as needed, to ensure their survival. Some due to their immediate circumstances, some do to the season of the year. Willow was sure that much of what must lie behind the reasons Mabel chose to change, was much the same as the reasons that animals had for changing. It was the need to survive in any circumstance, in the best way possible. For centuries, whether a person was a man or a woman had a great influence on their lives. To be able to change back and forth no doubt ensured Mabel of the ability to use the influence of his sex in many advantageous ways. And as a result, he ensured that in any circumstance he had the best possible chance to succeed. Actually, it was a very ingenious talent. 
It was this line of reasoning that enabled Willow to accept Mabel’s change. The reasoning enabled her to not only accept his change, but to appreciate it for the intelligence behind such an ability. Willow found Mabel’s ability to change just another insight into the psyche of the being sitting beside her. And this latest insight had the same affect on Willow as all the others she had so far discovered about Mabel. It fascinated her. 
“Shall we go swimming now?” Willow said, turning to look at Mabel. “I’m sure you’ll pick up the basics very rapidly.” She smiled into Mabel’s eyes. “Then we can swim out and I’ll show you some of the creatures we’ve developed.” 
“OK.” Mabel spoke with a slightly surprised look on her face. She had expected Willow to be upset at her appearance, or at least disconcerted for a while. But Willow was not. A small smile came into Mabel’s eyes. 
“It will be a pleasure.” Mabel spoke quietly. 
“Good.” Willow leaned over and quickly gave Mabel’s cheek a light kiss. “You know, you’re very beautiful this way.” Willow leaned back and grinned up into Mabel’s eyes. “But I think I prefer you as a man . . . just a tad more.” She laughed a small husky laugh. “I can’t imagine why . . . just a quirk on my part . . . no doubt.” 
“So . . .” Willow stood up and looked down at Mabel. “If later on today . . . it wouldn’t matter one way or the other to you . . . I’d love to have you become a man again.” She laughed. “It just seems like I can think of more things to do with you like that . . . although the ‘why’ escapes me now. But I’m sure . . . when you change back . . . I’ll remember all the details.” 
“I’ll keep that in mind.” Mabel slowly rose to join her. “I promise to turn back into a man soon. It just seemed we would see more of your ocean . . . if I toured it like this.” Mabel brushed a bit of long hair out of her eyes. “Sometimes it’s too easy to get distracted around you . . . the other way.” 
“You know.” Willow started to walk into the surf. “I think you’re right.” 
Mabel followed Willow out into the surf. The feeling of the water, surging around her and pulling at her as it returned to the ocean, was a pleasant new feeling. When the water became deep enough, Willow showed Mabel some of the basics of swimming. Making sure she had the hang of them, before they continued out into the deeper parts of the ocean. Slowly in the early morning light, the two of them made their way out to the coral reefs at the very edge of their horizon. There Willow knew they would find the creatures of beauty that inhabited their ocean. 

* * *

Lab was deserted when Maynard entered. Just as he had hoped, the others weren’t up and around yet. He didn’t bother to request that the lights be turned on. He just made his way quickly to the couch and sat down. After all his effort to hurry over here, he spent the next few moments just sitting motionless. He knew it was important to try to view the solution to his problem from all angles. Even though he was in a hurry as time was of the essence, he still didn’t want to overlook anything. 
“Amelia.” Maynard spoke quietly into the dimly lit darkness that surrounded him. The only light was coming in through the windows and it was only very early morning light. 
“Yes, Maynard.” Amelia answered. 
“Do you have a few minutes to talk to me?” Maynard asked. They usually conducted their research by using a workstation. It was very rare that he talked at length with Amelia like this.
“Yes.” Amelia answered.
“Good.” Maynard went on quietly. “Time effects what I’m going to talk to you about, so I must make my explanation as short as possible. Also the circumstances I find myself in are new and a bit confusing. I hope you’ll know that I’m doing my best.” 
“I’ve never known you not to be at your best.” Amelia replied helpfully.
“This will be a bit blunt.” Maynard paused. “But . . . as I said . . . time is important.”
“Yes. I understand that.” Amelia assured him. 
“I spent last night with Mabel learning how to be a bedmate.” Maynard paused again, which was very unusual for him. He was usually very succinct. “Mabel told me this morning that I had learned well . . . that my instructions were complete. But . . . I find myself with a very difficult problem to solve.” 
“I see. Go on.” Amelia encouraged. 
“It has been my ongoing desire to learn about humans and AIs as you well know. Since both species are frequently either engaged in sex or talking about sex or seemingly thinking about sex, I thought becoming a bedmate would be a good way to learn some answers. So . . . this basic logic encouraged me to make the recent changes in myself. And last night I did succeed in learning a great deal about sex.”
“I’m glad that you’ve been able to learn more. What did you learn?” Amelia asked. 
“Well . . . right now . . . I don’t have the time to tell you. But I will as soon as I can.” Maynard said in an apologetic tone. 
“After what I experienced last night, I find I need a sex partner. I’ve managed to learn why humans and AIs put so much of their energy into the subject of sex. And as an outcome of my experience, I find that the prospect of not continuing to engage in sex is disagreeable.” Maynard paused. He wanted very much to have Amelia understand his predicament. “You know how much I like cheese?” 
“Yes, you do seem to be enjoying yourself immensely when you eat cheese.” Amelia answered him. 
“Yes. Well . . . I find I like sex even more.” A small smile tugged at the corners of Maynard’s finely formed mouth. “The intricacies and possibilities which could exist in a sexual relationship are quite numerous. In fact, they could very well be virtually endless. The resulting knowledge that could be gained in the experience of a continued practice of sex makes me want to experience it more. But I find the most important factor and the most surprising factor for me in leading me to desire to find a sex partner, is that basically sex feels good. Damn good.”
“I see.” Amelia had never heard Maynard swear before and it gave her some small indication as to how deeply he felt about sex. “Your problem then . . . is the lack of a sex partner?” 
“Yes. Mabel will be returning to her Tavern soon and I’m sure if the human females on board desired a bedmate, they would’ve created one long ago.” Maynard answered quietly. 
“Yes. I can see that if you want to participate in sex for further study and knowledge or for pleasure, you’re lacking a fundamental part needed to accomplish that.” Amelia commented quietly.
“That’s precisely right.” Maynard was relieved that Amelia understood. “So . . . I’ve been thinking how I could get a sex partner.” 
“You could create one.” Amelia said helpfully. “I’m sure . . . when you explain the research already gained and the research you’ll gain from further study and the added need of pleasure, that Rowan will supply you with the raw materials. He has always been very generous in the past when we asked for the resources needed for our projects.”
“Yes. I’m sure he would supply me with the resources. And I could create a sex partner for myself. The idea had occurred to me. But I’ve come up with an even better solution.” Maynard paused, waiting a fraction of a second. Great importance rested on his next words. 
“You could create a body for yourself . . . and you could be my sex partner.” Maynard spoke hesitantly. 
“Oh . . . I see.” Amelia was silent for a few seconds. “If I created a female body for myself, it would solve your problem of a sex partner.” 
“Not only that.” Maynard hurriedly added as he stood up and began to pace the floor. “It would give us a chance to explore a sexual relationship together. With the data we could collect, we would learn a great deal more about AIs. In addition, some of our findings could possibly be representative of human relationships, at least representative enough to give us ideas that could then be elaborated on with further observations and questions involving humans.” Maynard paused. When he continued, his voice was more intense and hesitant. 
“I’d much rather . . . learn about sex . . . with someone I already know. Maintaining a sexual relationship . . . could be very complex. If I was involved with someone I already know . . . any information to be gained would be easier to acquire and understand.” Maynard stopped pacing. 
He found himself staring absently out the window. It was very important to him that Amelia understand how important it was to him to share this project with her. When he spoke again, he chose his words very carefully. 
“Amelia . . . from my limited experience with sex so far, I’ve found that it’s a very . . . powerful force. It has changed me in ways . . . I haven’t had the time to explore yet. It would seem to be a much less formidable prospect . . . if the person I was attempting to maintain a sexual relationship with . . . was someone I understand and who understands me.” 
“I can see the logic in that.” Amelia’s tone of voice changed slightly. “I can also see additional benefits for myself as well . . . benefits beyond the added information for our research.” Amelia’s voice paused, and then continued in a hesitant fashion.
“Since I have realized . . . that there are beings such as Mabel . . . that manage to transfer a ship-wide management system and personality . . . into an independent body . . . I’ve wondered at the possibility.” Amelia spoke quietly.
“Yes. I had thought of that additional advantage for you as well.” Maynard commented. “The possibility of your having an independent body would be a wonderful advancement for you.” He paused. “If you’re agreeable to making the changes, we’ll have to find out if Mabel will agree to help us. Failing that, you and I will be forced to try and stumble upon the solution ourselves.” 
“Yes. We would need Mabel’s help . . . if we’re going to accomplish this at all quickly. I see why you said time is important. We must see if Mabel will help us, before she returns to her Tavern. I’ll contact her and see if she’ll meet us here at once.” From Amelia’s tone of voice, Maynard could tell that she was growing excited about the possibility presented to her. 
“Wait.” Maynard spoke quickly. “Let me go to the beach and ask her myself. She was going to take a swim there with Willow and they may not want to be interrupted. I can wait until they’re finished and then ask her. If I ask Mabel in person, it will also give me the opportunity to explain my reasons.” He paused. “I wish we had something to offer Mabel in return. She seems to enjoy bargaining. If we had something to offer her, she might be more likely to help us.” Maynard reasoned. 
“Your plan to contact Mabel personally sounds like it has the best chance for success.” Amelia paused. “We have our research to offer Mabel. We have already formulated many ideas and opinions that are valued on the Net. The information we’ll gain as sex partners will be valuable knowledge. It might be a bargaining chip worth trying.” Amelia said helpfully. “While you’re asking for Mabel’s help, I’m going to research more thoroughly what my changes might involve. Please, let me know the moment you have news.” 
“Yes. I will.” Maynard answered. “And . . . Amelia . . . thank you for helping me solve my problem . . . I’m very grateful.” 
“You’re most welcome.” Amelia acknowledged. “If we manage to find a way to solve your problem by making me into an independent person, I’ll have much to thank you for as well.” 
“I’ll talk to you later, then.” Maynard said as he left Lab. 
“Good luck.” He heard Amelia say as the door closed behind him. 

* * *

When Maynard arrived at the beach, he could just make out the forms of Willow and Mabel swimming far out at the very edge of the ship’s horizon. He sank down onto the sand, leaning his back against a palm tree. Closing his eyes, he remained motionless, feeling the breeze on his arms and legs. Feeling the breeze like this was one of the new pleasures he had experienced since he took on his furless form. 
He was pleased that Amelia was agreeable to becoming the solution to his problem. The only problem he had left to solve was how to get Mabel to agree to help them. Without Mabel’s help it would take Amelia and him a long time to figure out how to transform Amelia into an independent person, if they could discover the way at all. As smart and knowledgeable as he and Amelia were, Mabel had already done it. It was much more efficient to try to get Mabel to help them, than to attempt to figure it out for themselves. 
Maynard sat there in the shade of the palm tree, and patiently waited for Willow and Mabel to finish their swim. He found that his time was more than adequately taken up with contemplating how to convince Mabel to help them. 
“So . . . sleeping on the job?” Willow’s voice interrupted his thoughts. 
“No . . . just thinking.” Maynard replied, opening his eyes to find Willow and Mabel standing in front of him stark naked and dripping wet from the sea. And they were both women. 
“Ah . . . did you ladies enjoy your swim?” Maynard found to his surprise that the sight of naked women affected him much differently then it had when he had been a teddy bear. It was going to take some time to get used to this.
“Yes, we did.” Mabel commented. “Did you want to join us?” 
“No . . . but thanks.” Maynard replied. “I was just waiting for a chance to speak with you. I didn’t want to interrupt.” 
“Well . . . I believe we’re done. Aren’t we . . . Willow?” Mabel asked her. 
“Yes . . . at least until you change back into a man.” Willow grinned at her. “I’m off to the showers. I’ll see you later. And . . . remember . . . you promised to change back into a man. You know I can’t proceed with my quest . . . with you like this.” Willow said, waving as she started walking down the beach towards the Beach Shack showers. 
“I guess Willow likes you better as a man.” Maynard commented as Mabel sat down on the sand next to him. 
“Yes, she does. I must admit . . . I was pleasantly surprised at how she handled my being a woman this morning.” Mabel watched Willow striding down the beach. “She’s an amazing woman.” 
“Yes, she is.” Maynard agreed. He paused as his eyes found their way unbidden to Mabel’s beautiful shape, lingering there for a long moment. 
“Before I talk to you . . . would you mind putting on some clothes? I find that I have a different reaction to naked women than I used to before my change.” Maynard spoke quietly.
“Yes . . . I can see that.” Mabel teased. “I’ll do even better than that. Where can I meet you in an hour, where we can talk? I’ll show up as a man, thereby making both you and Willow happy.” 
“You could meet me in Lab. I’ll go there now and wait for you.” Maynard stood up.
