“Yes.” Laurel kissed him. “I do value a good man. I’ve had the pleasure of learning from you . . . just what a good man is.” She smiled at him. “How could I not create characteristics in Maynard that mirrored the goodness in you?” 
“God.” Laurel turned back around and sank down into the tub again. “I’m sure lucky . . . to have two good men that care about me.” 
“Yes.” Rowan kissed the top of her head. “You’re very lucky.” He paused. “And so am I.” He paused again, choosing his words carefully. “It’s hard enough as it is . . . to know that you’ve just made love with someone else. I’m very grateful to know that the person you’ve chosen . . . is someone as caring as Maynard.” 
“It’s going to be OK.” Laurel spoke softly, taking his hand from where it rested lovingly on her breast and kissing it gently. “I love you.” 
“Yes . . . I know you do.” Rowan paused. “But still . . . it isn’t easy.” 
“I know.” Laurel replied slowly. “I just hope when it’s my turn to know that you and Amelia have made love . . . that I’ll take it as well as you are.” She paused. “But I’ll let you know ahead of time . . . that when you and she finally get together . . . I’m going to need a lot of hugs to get through it.” 
“Yes. I know what you mean.” Rowan stood up and helped Laurel up. “Let’s go get in bed and go to sleep.” He paused and looked down into her caring eyes. “I think I could use a good supply of those kinds of hugs . . . about now.”
“OK.” Laurel hugged him and kissed him gently. “Let’s do that. And . . . if you think of anything else I can do to help you through this . . . just let me know.” 
They dried off and climbed into bed. Laurel lay down and Rowan lay by her side, his head resting on her breasts and her arms holding him tight. 
“I love you.” Laurel spoke softly, and then she kissed his forehead. Then she listened to Rowan’s breathing and held him tight, until he fell asleep. When Laurel was sure that he wouldn’t feel her hugs anymore, she released the tight squeeze she’d been holding him in. And with her arms still around him, she let herself fall asleep as well. 


## Chapter 9 — MOVES




Amelia spent much of the night after Rowan left her and most of the next morning dealing with ship’s business. Not long after Rowan had left, Amelia had been notified that the fleet of ships had managed to move the comet into its new orbit position. So she had situated herself at one of the workstations in the Beach Shack and begun the procedures necessary to accomplish the time-consuming task of coordinating the releasing of the comet in just the right place. 
After that Amelia had begun the process that would establish a station on the surface of the comet. The self-automated station would dispense the water contained in the comet to the ships that had helped move the comet. Amelia had done all the preparations she could ahead of time. But she wanted to oversee the beginning phases of the operation personally, to make sure that the Conbots were carrying out their construction duties according to plan. By the following night the station would be able to begin to distribute water to all the ships. 
It was mid-morning before Amelia could leave the console. She had noticed when Maynard had returned right before dawn. He had entered cautiously, and when he found her alone, he had spoken a greeting. He had showered and gotten dressed in some clean clothes and then left again to go work on something in Lab. 
Amelia left the console and went to the window to look out on the world that was still so new to her. She decided to shower and join Maynard in Lab. She could be reached there if she were needed for further help with the Water Resource Distribution Station. 
After showering, Amelia changed into another of the outfits Juniper had created for her. This outfit was a flight suit designed with slight differences, a bit more form fitting than the first and made of a shimmering material. Amelia paused to straighten up the Beach Shack before she left. 
Amelia had just gone out the door and closed it behind her, when she saw a balloon floating in the air. The balloon was tethered by a ribbon to the deck chair that she and Rowan had been sitting in the night before while watching the stars. Amelia walked over to take a closer look. The balloon was a light pink color and inside it there appeared to be a rose. She reached out to move the balloon closer so she could see it better. The instant her fingers touched the ribbon, the balloon burst, filling the air around her with the delicate fragrance of fresh roses. 
When the balloon burst, the rose and what appeared to be a card fell to land on the seat of the deck chair. Amelia bent and picked them up. The rose was alive. The card was gold-edged and written in gold-etched script. 
“Amelia.” The card read in large fancy letters at the top. “I would be very honored if you would agree to accompany me to the celebration to be held at Mabel’s. Yours, Rowan.” 
A smile spread slowly across Amelia’s face. When she twirled the rose, another concentrated burst of fragrance filled the air. Amelia bent down to smell the fragrance more closely, even though the faint fragrance that had come from the burst balloon still filled the air around her.
She knew Rowan had personal systems that didn’t connect directly with the ship, not unless he wanted to transfer something or contact her. He must have used one of those systems to create this without her knowledge. Amelia’s smile grew. She had knowledge about the phenomenon called romance, but this was her first personal exposure to it. It was nice. She found she rather liked it. 
Amelia carefully put the note in the pocket of her flight suit. She picked up the remnants of the popped balloon and recycled them. Then she left for Lab. She walked along every now and then twirling the rose in her hand so she could once again drink in its delicate fragrance. And every time she twirled the rose, a smile lit her eyes. 
When Amelia arrived at Lab, Maynard was busy at one of the worktables. She walked in and said “good morning” and took a place at one of the other worktables to work on the first steps towards creating the sleep program. But before she started her work, Amelia had the Lab Dispenser create a small delicate vase to hold her rose. Only after placing the rose in the vase and giving it a place of honor on her worktable, did she began the challenging work that faced her. 
It was noon when Rowan and Laurel entered Lab. Laurel went over to Maynard to kiss him lightly on the cheek and see what he was doing. She was a bit surprised to see him quickly putting away whatever it was he had been working on. She was too late to see anything more than a few small instruments being hastily put into a drawer. Laurel kissed him lightly on the cheek, bending down to whisper in his ear. When Maynard turned to grin at her, Laurel smiled back and kissed him. 
Rowan and Laurel had come to Lab to get an update on the comet moving situation. Laurel was just leaving Maynard to catch up to Rowan, when she saw Rowan reach the worktable where Amelia was working. 
At Rowan’s approach, Amelia got up and turned to smile at him as light danced in her eyes. Amelia reached out and hugged Rowan, while whispering a soft “yes” and “thank you.” into his ear. Then she sat down again and began to fill Rowan in on the latest on the comet, every once in a while reaching out to twirl the rose in the vase on her worktable. 
Laurel very easily put two and two together. One look at Rowan and Amelia’s faces told her at least the important part of what lay behind Amelia’s actions. Laurel smiled to herself. The romancing must have started. She didn’t want to ponder too long on what lay ahead for Amelia. She knew that as brave as she was, it wouldn’t be a good idea for her to contemplate the details. So without delaying any further, Laurel went to join Rowan at the worktable to hear the latest news of the comet. 
The rest of the day passed quickly. Amelia and Maynard began to work with Rowan on the sleep program. Laurel found that she was even able to help occasionally. 
In the afternoon Rowan called a meeting in Group to which he asked that only the men attend. He explained that Mabel, Rodjer, Maynard, and he wanted to plan the celebration that would take place at Mabel’s Tavern the following evening. And they wanted their plans to be a surprise. 
Since the comet was now securely in its new orbit and the Water Resource Distribution Station was almost completed, each ship would be supplied with the comet’s water by the following evening. As yet Amelia hadn’t received a single word from the United Earth Governments about the removal of the comet from their Safety Zone. Amelia had predicted that the United Earth Governments would probably just ignore anything more relating in any way to the comet because they no longer had any legal way to interfere. The comet no longer threatened anyone and it was now situated out of their jurisdiction. So far the United Earth Governments seemed to be doing just as Amelia predicted and that was a relief to everyone concerned. 
While the men held their celebration-planning meeting in Group, the women took the opportunity to gather in Juniper’s dome to design special outfits to wear to the celebration. And just as before when they had spent time helping Amelia with her wardrobe, the afternoon and evening passed quickly. 
The off-loading of water from the comet to the ships was started that night. Amelia and Maynard stayed in Lab all night. It was important to Amelia to be on hand to oversee the beginning of the water distribution. She wanted to make sure that the Water Resource Distribution Station performed as expected, independently distributing water to the ships that had helped move the comet. The ships would be able to return to the comet whenever they wanted for more water. After Amelia had assured herself that the Water Resource Distribution Station was working perfectly, she and Maynard put the rest of the night to good use working on their sleep program. 

* * *

Mabel and Willow, with Keeko nestled in Willow’s arms, left the ship early the following morning when the Recon from Mabel’s Tavern came to pick them up. Willow didn’t have to answer in-depth questions about her departure with Mabel. Everyone just assumed she was going along to help with the celebration preparations, which she was. Only she and Mabel knew of her ulterior motive in accompanying him. 
Most of the morning and afternoon was spent by the other inhabitants of the ship in a flurry of activity. The activity seemed to be centered mainly on Juniper’s dome as she rose to the challenge of designing formal attire for the men. 
Now the late afternoon sun shone in through the windows of their dome and alighted briefly on Rowan as he stood in front of the mirror adjusting his black bow tie. He looked very handsome in his tux and tails. The cut of the tux accentuated his build. The fabric of the tux and the cut of the tails were pure elegance itself. Laurel had commented often while he dressed that he looked stunning. And she continued to do so at frequent intervals. 
“God . . . you’re gorgeous.” Laurel came up behind Rowan and gave him a light hug. She was very careful not to wrinkle his tux. “You look good enough to eat.” 
“I do, do I?” Rowan turned away from the mirror with a grin. “Well . . . before I recycle this tux, I’ll have to wear it once more. We’ll have a little celebration of our own.” He kissed Laurel lightly. “You look great.” And she did. She was dressed in only a pair of black lace panties. 
“I hope you plan to wear a bit more to the celebration.” Rowan reached out and gave her butt an affectionate squeeze. “Otherwise . . . I’m not going to be able to keep my hands off you.” 
“Well . . .” Laurel grinned at Rowan. “Just because you’re taking someone else to the party . . . doesn’t mean you can’t sneak a feel now and then.” She kissed his cheek. 
“I’ll keep that in mind.” Rowan laughed. 
Gathering up a beautiful bouquet of fresh roses and a box of candy, Rowan returned to give Laurel a goodbye kiss. 
“See you later.” Rowan said after the lengthy kiss. “I’m off to pick up my date.” 
“Well . . .” Laurel spoke, taking in the sight of him as he walked to the door. “All I can say is . . . she’s one lucky lady.” 
“Laurel.” Rowan paused on his way to the door. He turned and came back to her. “You’re all right . . . aren’t you?” He paused. “I probably won’t see you alone again until tomorrow morning sometime.” He searched her face. “Are you going to be OK?”
“Well . . .” Laurel managed a bit of a lopsided smile. “If I walk up to you at the party and ask for a hug . . . don’t worry. Just hug me and whisper in my ear that you love me . . . and I’ll be fine.” She kissed him. “I might need just a little reassurance.” 
“Anytime.” Rowan smiled into her eyes. “There are lots of hugs waiting for you in my arms anytime you need them.” He kissed her again. 
“Thanks.” Laurel summoned up a grin. “Now . . . off with you. You have a date to collect and I’ve got to finish dressing. I’m late.” She laughed a small laugh. “It would be interesting though . . . to just wear this . . . and watch you drool all evening trying to keep your hands off me.” She teased him. 
“Who me? Keep my hands off you?” Rowan teased back. “Never. I’d just bundle both you and Amelia off . . . and have my way with both of you at once.” 
“You would, too . . . I bet.” Laurel called to his retreating back as he disappeared out the door. 
Rowan was just a few steps down the path towards the beach, when he met Maynard coming down the path towards him. 
“Hello.” Maynard greeted Rowan. 
“Hi. I see you’ve got your ‘monkey suit’ on as well.” Rowan paused to comment on Maynard’s elegant tux and tails. 
“Yes.” Maynard replied with a smile. “This is some fancy party we’re attending.” 
“Only the best.” Rowan returned his smile. “See you there.” He said over his shoulder as he continued on towards the beach. 
Rowan went a bit farther down the path, and then he paused and turned around to look at the receding back of the man that was going to escort Laurel to the party. Before he could think too much about how strange all this seemed, he hurried on his way. 

* * *

Maynard smoothed the elegant fabric of his tux and made sure his tails were in place. Then he knocked on the door to the dome. From inside Laurel heard his knock and panicked slightly. Her escort was here and she wasn’t anywhere near ready. 
“Is that you, Maynard?” Laurel called out loudly.
“Yes.” Maynard replied from outside. “Can I come in?” 
“No.” Laurel called out hastily. “I’m not ready yet.” 
“Well . . . could I come in and help?” Maynard asked hopefully. 
“OK.” Laurel called out. “But you’ve got to promise to help . . . and not hinder me in getting ready.” 
“I promise.” Maynard answered as he opened the door. And he instantly regretted his promise. For when he opened the door, Laurel stood in the middle of the dome in nothing but a pair of black lace panties. 
“God . . .” Maynard spoke hesitantly as he stood in the doorway. “Can I take that promise back?” He grinned at her. “You look too gorgeous.” 
“No.” Laurel said firmly as she turned and left him standing in the doorway. “If you come in . . . you have to help me get dressed. Not the other way around. I’m late.” 
“I can see that.” Maynard walked in. “I promise . . . against my better judgment. I’ll help you get dressed.” 
“Thanks.” Laurel turned to smile at him from where she was gathering up sundry items. “Sorry . . . I’m so late. I didn’t realize it was quite so late until Rowan left.”
“Well . . . what can I do to help?” Maynard asked carefully, gazing at her with unrestrained lust. 
“You can get my perfume off the shelf over there.” Laurel ignored his leer with difficulty. 
“OK . . . here’s your perfume.” Maynard brought it over and handed it to her.
“Thanks.” Laurel sprayed a bit on the inside of each of her wrists.
She sprayed some on each side of her neck right below her ears. Then she proceeded to spray some under each of her breasts. Maynard groaned a low, lusty groan. Laurel looked up quickly. 
“Ah . . . I can’t take this.” Maynard paused. “I know . . . I promised.” He said as Laurel started to comment. “I’ll just go way over there and sit on the bed . . . while you finish. I think it might be a bit easier on me over there . . . but not much.” He looked doubtfully back at her where she stood semi-clad and smelling sensational. 
Laurel turned her back on him and started to put on her black nylons, the ones with delicate seams up the back. When she asked if her seams were straight, she only got another groan in return. She took that for an affirmative answer and continued to dress. She put on her short little black crushed velvet skirt, twirling once in front of the mirror to make sure the skirt moved just right. Another groan came from Maynard’s direction. 
Next Laurel put on the shimmering black lingerie top that Juniper had designed. The top clung to her, molding itself around her breasts. It was strapless. And by some miracle having to do with the fabric, it clung to her. No amount of moving around or dancing would cause it to loose its hold. She had tried. 
Lastly Laurel put on her delicate silver necklace with a crystal embedded in its intricate design. Before Rowan had left, she had finished with her make up and hair and earrings. Her make up was bold and shimmering tonight. The earrings matched the necklace’s delicate design and her hair was as she loved it, very long and very wild. 
“All done.” Laurel said as she slipped her feet into her high heels. Thank God Juniper had come up with a truly comfortable way to make high heels. It had been one of her first fashion innovations. 
“Very nice.” Maynard spoke from where he sat on the bed as she came over. He paused and studied her carefully. “I’m a very lucky man.” 
“And I’m a very lucky lady to have such an elegant escort.” Laurel smiled at him as he stood and she took his arm to leave. 
Then Laurel’s eye caught a sparkle coming from the bed, a sparkle that hadn’t been there before. Laurel paused and looked more closely. Yes, there it was. She thought her eye had caught a gleam.
“Maynard!” Laurel yelled when she realized what had caught her eye. “Did you do this?” Laurel crawled onto the bed and grabbed up her teddy bear. She ran back to Maynard and jumped into his arms. 
Luckily for Laurel, Maynard caught her when she jumped. He ended up holding Laurel close as she straddled him with her legs and hugged him with her arms. She covered his face and neck with kisses, not to mention his mouth. Maynard was pleased. From her reaction he felt he could safely assume she liked his gift. 
After a few more moments of unabashedly showing Maynard her appreciation with more kisses, Laurel disentangled herself from him to look more closely at her teddy bear. It was the teddy bear she had made out of the bits and pieces of fur from the old Maynard. Hanging around the teddy bear’s neck was a delicately fashioned locket of gold. The locket was made up of a tiny framework of scrolled gold containing a picture of the old Maynard and herself. 
The picture looked as though it had been taken at the time when she had first created him. The old Maynard was smiling while she stood beside him, her arm around him and a smile on her face. Laurel turned the tiny locket over and a smile lit her eyes as she read the inscription. Engraved in delicate script were the words “Love . . . always and forever, Maynard”. 
Laurel looked from the locket in her hands to Maynard’s bronze face that was watching her. He had a strange look on his face, a very serious look and a bit of a sheepish look. 
“I love it.” Laurel’s voice and eyes were alight with happiness. 
“I’m glad.” Maynard replied softly. “I thought you might like it.”
“Oh . . . I do.” Laurel kissed the teddy bear’s nose and put it back on her bed.
“That was so sweet.” Laurel came back to Maynard and hugged him. “I can’t believe how sweet you are.” She reached up and kissed him gently. “Thank you.” 
Maynard didn’t say a word. He just gathered her up into his arms and hugged her close. 
“Now.” Maynard said firmly, releasing her. “We’ve got to get to Recon.” A grin lit his eyes as he looked down at her. “If we stay in this room a moment longer with you looking like you do . . . and being as grateful as you are . . . all promises are off.” 
“OK.” Laurel returned his grin and headed for the door. “But . . . hold that thought for later.” 
Maynard glanced quickly over to the bed where Laurel’s teddy bear sat in its place of honor. Then he turned and followed Laurel out into the late afternoon sunlight. 

* * *

Rowan approached the Beach Shack with his arms full of romantic gifts for Amelia. It had been a long time since he had picked up a woman for a date. Rowan knocked on the door of the Beach Shack and received a “come in” from inside. He opened the door slowly and walked inside. 
Amelia stood in the center of the room, ready and waiting for him. Her white gown shimmered in the late afternoon light. It clung to her form. The white sparkle of the gown seemed to make Amelia’s skin glow like alabaster. The gown was strapless and plunged deeply between her breasts. A long slit up one side exposed one very shapely leg from toes to thigh. 
“My God . . . you’re gorgeous.” Rowan murmured in a quiet voice. 
Amelia slowly began to do a pirouette in front of him. As she turned slowly, Rowan could see that the back of her gown plunged very low in the back as well. He could see the delicate slimness of the small of her back. Her long red hair hung full and thick down the naked expanse of her back. The vision took his breath away. 
“You are absolutely beautiful.” Rowan spoke as Amelia finished her pirouette.
“It’s going to be an even greater pleasure than I thought to escort you to the celebration tonight.” He moved forward to execute a perfectly elegant bow in front of her. He handed her the roses and the candy. 
“Thank you.” Amelia took the gifts he offered. 
Rowan sat down on the bed, watching as Amelia requested a large vase from the Dispenser. She placed all but one of the roses in the vase and set the vase on the table by the bed. Amelia brought the box of candy with her as she moved towards the bed.
Without saying a word, Amelia sat down beside Rowan. From looking at her gown, Rowan thought it would’ve been impossible for her to sit in it. It clung that tightly. But probably do to some special fabric creation of Juniper’s, Amelia was able to sit in the dress comfortably. The only change was that the slit up the side revealed a bit more of her gorgeous thigh. 
She opened the box of candy and selected a tasty morsel. Placing the bit of candy to his lips, she offered it to him. Rowan took the candy into his mouth as he kissed the tip of her fingers, a slight smile lighting his eyes. 
“This candy is delicious.” Amelia murmured as she tasted a piece herself.
Amelia reached out and offered another piece to Rowan. This time when she placed the candy to his lips, Rowan took the piece of candy as well as the tips of her fingers into his mouth. He sucked just briefly on the tips of Amelia’s fingers before releasing them, noticing as he did so that a small smile found its way onto the corners of her mouth. 
“Um . . .” Rowan commented under his breath. “That tastes good . . . and the candy ain’t bad either.” 
“Oh . . . you like that . . . do you?” Amelia grinned at him. 
“Yes.” Rowan answered quietly, his eyes twinkling at her. “I like that very much . . . definitely delicious. But next time . . . I’d like to try it . . . without the candy.” 
“That can be arranged.” Amelia replied, laughing. 
“Good.” Rowan got to his feet. “But right now, we’ve got to get to Recon.” Rowan paused for a moment to gaze at her. Amelia didn’t have any trouble figuring out what he was thinking. “It’d be a shame to waste such a beautiful gown . . . on only me.” 
“Oh . . . I don’t think it would be a waste . . . if you were the only one to see me in it.” Amelia spoke slowly, watching Rowan as she took the hand he offered and stood up to join him. Amelia reached out to pick up the single rose. “I think even if you were the only one to see me in this gown . . . it would still be well worth it.” 
“You’re right.” Rowan walked with Amelia towards the door. He leaned down and kissed her. “I think I could show my appreciation in such a way . . . that you wouldn’t miss not having any other admiring looks.” 
“I’m sure of it.” Amelia replied as they closed the Beach Shack door behind them. “I haven’t a single doubt about that.” 
Rowan placed Amelia’s hand in the crook of his arm and they walked down the path towards where Recon was docked. Amelia walked beside Rowan every now and then twirling the single rose she carried in her other hand. 

* * *

Mabel sat on the couch, watching Willow dress. They were in the room Mabel had designed especially for Willow. It was a room that consisted mainly of crystal and light. The couch on which Mabel sat was a crystal affair with lavender light emanating from the center of its base and radiating outwards. Mabel sat on what would appear to be cushions of soft purple light. The force contained in the light held him secure and comfortable in its grasp. He leaned back, watching carefully as Willow finished preparing herself for the celebration that was about to start out in the Tavern. 
Willow moved around the room. The pillars of crystal and the columns of light flashed in and around her, creating a splendid effect of dancing colors. There were all variations of the color purple blended and moving just so, to create an enveloping work of art in which Willow moved. 
Of course Willow had loved the room when Mabel had first shown it to her hours ago. They had spent the morning making the arrangements for the party, making sure that everything was in readiness. Then in the early afternoon hours, Mabel had brought her here. Willow had exclaimed amazed at the creations he had made in her honor, marveling at his abilities to create shape and motion. And Mabel had enjoyed her reaction. 
Much to his amazement, Mabel found that not only did he enjoy bringing pleasure to this woman he also enjoyed just being around her. The way Willow reacted to something was not at all the way he had observed that most people reacted. Willow observed everything around herself in a way unlike anyone he had ever met. 
She picked up on subtleties that Mabel saw, but didn’t think important. His view of what he thought was unimportant changed when Willow highlighted them with thoughts and words based on her own unique way of looking at them. Mabel’s environment took on a new twist when viewed through Willow’s eyes. He valued the way she was able to view the world, it was a view that was so unlike his own. 
They had spent a very enjoyable afternoon. Now, once again, Mabel watched as Willow attempted yet again to get ready for the party. They had a way of getting sidetracked. Once again, Willow brushed her long hair. It rippled and flowed becoming a thing of beauty. 
“Could you brush it a bit from behind?” Willow asked him with a smile. 
“Sure.” Mabel answered, getting up. He found he loved to brush her hair. 
Willow stood still while Mabel brushed her hair slowly, his eyes catching her reflection in the mirror. 
“You’re beautiful.” Mabel spoke in a low voice while his hands smoothed her long strands of luscious hair. Then as so often before his hands wandered on beyond her hair, reaching down to feel the smooth silk of her purple panties. 
Willow turned around to smile at him, reaching up to encircle him with her arms. 
“Now . . . here . . . just as I thought I had almost succeeded in getting dressed.” Willow smiled up into Mabel’s eyes. “I thought for sure . . . this time I’d succeed. After all, it’s getting late.” 
“I know, fair lady.” Mabel grinned down at her. “But . . . it’s not easy to see you and keep my hands off.” 
“Look at me.” Willow laughed. “I’m all ready. All I have to do is slip into my dress.” 
“I am looking at you.” Mabel laughed a twinkle in his dark eyes. “That’s the problem. You look so gorgeous.” He leaned down and kissed her. Then he backed away from her, taking her hand gently and kissing it. “No matter how many times my body fills you with my desire, fair lady . . . my spirit still desires more.” 
“You do have a way with words, Mabel.” Willow’s eyes danced as she watched his face. “I just despair of ever getting dressed.” She gazed into his eyes, an attempted grimace on her face. “This isn’t the first time . . . I’ve tried. For some reason . . . I always manage to get sidetracked.” 
“The fault is entirely mine, my dear.” Mabel said, a leer coming into his eyes. “How about . . . if this time . . . I show you my desire here?” Mabel gently lifted her up until she was sitting on the counter. “Just one more time before your dress descends to cover up this delicious sight.” Mabel grinned as he pulled Willow close. 
“I promise not to mess you up too much.” He laughed. “I’ll just slide in and out without disturbing your attire more than a tiny bit.” Mabel paused and a long sigh escaped him. “There . . . how’s that?” Mabel asked quietly. “I didn’t disturb your attire much at all . . . did I?” 
“Just like . . . I always suspected.” Willow smiled up at him. “You’re a very . . . talented man.” 
“Oh . . . that was nothing.” Mabel said with a grin. “Just wait . . .” 
But Willow didn’t have to wait for more evidence of his talent. He was already showing her. 

* * *

Six very elegant people disembarked from Recon. They found the corridors leading towards the Tavern filled with various people in similarly elegant attire. They joined the streams of people heading towards the Tavern, enjoying the feeling of excitement in the air. Juniper held Rodjer’s hand and snuck looks at both Amelia and Laurel. She was immensely pleased with the way they looked in her creations. 
Juniper had chosen a very simple outfit for herself. She wore a short tight mini skirt with a very tight top, both sparkling pink. At first glance, her outfit looked simple. But it wasn’t. At various intervals the color of the outfit would change as new waves of color flowed through the outfit to replace the old. The effect was random and very appealing. Juniper’s entire jewelry ensemble was made up of delicate forms of crystal. No matter what color her outfit became, the crystals always reflected that same color. The effect was dazzling. Rodjer wore an elegant black tux of a design not unlike the other men present. When the effect of the tux was combined with Rodjer’s blonde good looks, he made a very handsome escort for the ever changing Juniper. 
When the elegant group of six reached the place where all the corridors converged together, they found Mabel and Willow greeting each of the guests before the guests proceeded through the double doors into the Tavern. Mabel wore an elegant tux and tails and Willow wore a dress of black and purple. It flowed and moved in a way that caused it to seem to float around her. 
“Welcome to Mabel’s.” Mabel greeted the elegant group of six with a smile. “I hope you each enjoy the festivities.” Mabel handed each of the men a gold key. Willow greeted each of them warmly, gesturing them on in through the double doors for there were many more guests for them to greet. Each man in the elegant group of six slipped the gold key into his pocket as they escorted the ladies through the double doors. 
The Tavern had been transformed. It wasn’t at all as Laurel and Rowan and Maynard remembered it from their first visit. On the other side of the double doors, the elegant group of six found that they had entered an elegant ballroom. Sparkling stars and a brilliant full moon in the domed ceiling provided the room with light. The entire ambiance of the huge room was crystal and silver. “No.” Amelia answered. “We’re resting now.” 
“Good.” Maynard sent. “I need your help to solve a problem with Laurel. She’s upset . . . very upset. As I’m communicating with you, she’s crying in my arms. I suggested that I go and get Rowan, but she wouldn’t let me. She’s determined not to disturb him.” 
There was a slight pause, and then Amelia sent her reply. 
“I’ve just informed Rowan about what you’ve just told me. He’s contacting Mabel as I’m relaying this to you, to find out how to get to your room.” 
“Good.” Maynard sent in return. “In the meantime, could you talk to Laurel through me? Maybe you can help me figure out what’s wrong.” 
“OK.” Amelia answered. “You better set it up from your end.” 
“OK.” Maynard said. 
He shifted the position of Laurel in his arms so he could see her better. 
“Laurel.” Maynard began quietly. “I want to talk to you.” 
“I don’t . . . want to . . . talk . . . right now.” Laurel mumbled softly. 
“I know. But I want to talk to you.” Maynard continued. “Please.” 
“OK.” Laurel sat up and looked at him. 
“Good girl.” Maynard smiled at her. “I want you to talk to Amelia.” 
“What!” Laurel said surprised. 
“It’s OK.” Maynard tried to calm her. “I’m in communication with Amelia right now . . . and I think she may be able to help you.” 
“You’re in contact with Amelia now?” Laurel asked surprised. 
“Yes.” Maynard answered. “Amelia will talk to you through me.” Then Maynard adjusted his hearing and speech centers, so Amelia could use them as well.
“Laurel.” Amelia spoke quietly. “Rowan has just left our room to come to yours. He should be there in about five minutes. It seems that your room is located quite a distance from ours.” 
Laurel was flabbergasted. To hear Amelia’s voice come out of Maynard’s mouth was outrageous. But her concern at having interrupted Amelia and Rowan, won out over her surprise at hearing Amelia’s voice come from such an unusual source. 
“Oh . . . God . . . no.” Laurel murmured softly. “I didn’t mean to interrupt you. I told Maynard not to get Rowan. Oh . . . God.” 
“It’s all right.” Amelia’s calming voice tried to sooth Laurel. “Maynard really had no choice. Besides . . . Maynard didn’t interrupt us. We were resting.” 
“Well . . . thank goodness for that at least.” Laurel was a bit relieved. “But . . . I’ve still interrupted your evening. God . . . I wish Maynard hadn’t bothered you.” 
“I’m sorry . . . Laurel.” Maynard’s voice spoke quietly. “I just couldn’t sit by and do nothing to help you.” 
“I know.” Laurel leaned forward and kissed his cheek as she looked into his sad eyes. “It wasn’t fair of me to expect you’d do anything else.” She attempted a small smile. “It’s OK. I just feel very strange having caused you to interrupt their evening.”
“It’s all right . . . really.” Amelia assured her through Maynard. “I’m more concerned right now with what is making you so upset. Can you talk to me about it?” 
“Oh God . . . I suppose. I’ve really managed to mess things up now.” Laurel stifled a sob. “I guess I should try to explain.” She paused. “I just couldn’t help thinking about you and Rowan . . . and what you were doing.” 
“Is that what made you so upset . . . thoughts about what Rowan and I were doing?” Amelia asked quietly. 
“Well . . . I guess it wasn’t so much that. It was more the fact that I missed Rowan.” Laurel sniffled loudly. “I just couldn’t help thinking about what you both were doing . . . and well . . . I just wanted Rowan. But . . . I couldn’t interrupt him. I had to wait until morning to see Rowan . . . and to talk to him. Then . . . the time went by so slow . . . and all I could do was think about what you both were doing . . . and I just started to cry. Maynard tried to help . . . but he couldn’t make it be morning . . . so I could talk to Rowan. So . . . I just cried . . . and I think it hurt Maynard’s feelings . . . and I . . . I’m sorry . . . I’m not handling this . . . very well.” Laurel sank down on the bed, crying. 
“I see.” Amelia answered while Maynard stroked Laurel’s back. “Well . . . what we’ve got to do is get you calmed down enough to talk this out. Rowan should be there soon.”
“Oh God . . . no.” Laurel cried. “How am I going to face him? I’ve wrecked his evening . . . and he’s going to be so disappointed . . . that I wasn’t able to handle all this better.”
“No . . . he won’t.” Amelia spoke quietly. “He’ll understand. We all will . . . once you calm down enough to explain what’s wrong.” 
“Oh . . . you all must really be . . . upset with me.” Laurel cried, her voice muffled by a pillow. “I’ve managed to mess up everything.” She paused and a sob racked her body. “I just needed Rowan.” She sobbed out. “He said I could get a hug . . . whenever I needed one . . . but when I needed one . . . he was with you. I couldn’t interrupt him. But . . . I needed a hug . . . from him . . . so bad.” 
Laurel buried her head in the pillow and sobbed. 
“It’s all right.” Amelia assured her quietly. “Rowan will be there soon.” 
Laurel didn’t say anything more. She just lay on Maynard’s lap and sobbed into the pillow. 
“Maynard.” Amelia communicated to him. “Rowan should be there any minute.” 
“Good.” Maynard sent in return. “Laurel’s just laying here on my lap crying.” 
“I know.” Amelia answered. “I can hear.” 
“I’m stroking her hair and holding her.” Maynard said. “That’s all I can think of to do.” 
“That’s about all you can do until Rowan gets there.” Amelia answered. 
There was a hurried knock on the door to their room. Maynard immediately called “come in” and Rowan ran into the room. He was dressed in one of the robes that Mabel supplied in every room. His dark hair was in disarray and his face was worried. Rowan’s eyes immediately sought out Laurel and he ran to her, climbing onto the bed and scooping her up into his arms to hold her close. 
“I’m . . . so . . . sorry.” Laurel cried into Rowan’s shoulder. “I’m . . . so sorry.” 
“It’s OK.” Rowan repeated over and over. “It’s OK.” 
“No . . . it’s not.” Laurel cried. “I’ve messed everything up.” 
“No . . . you haven’t.” Rowan assured her. 
“Amelia.” Maynard sent as soon as Rowan arrived. “Rowan is here.” 
“Good.” Amelia returned. “I want to talk to him.” 
“Rowan.” Maynard spoke quietly. “Amelia wants to talk to you. She can communicate with you through me.” 
“Oh . . .” Rowan said in a surprised voice. When he thought about it, his only surprise was that he’d been surprised at all. He’d known the possibility existed.
“Rowan.” Amelia said through Maynard. “I think I know why Laurel is so upset . . . at least a good part of it.” 
“You do?” Rowan spoke quietly, thinking aloud. “Well . . . maybe Maynard and I should bring Laurel back to our room. I think it might be a good idea . . . if we sort all this out together.” 
“I agree.” Amelia said. 
“Laurel.” Rowan spoke softly. “Can you try to stop crying now? I need to know whether you need to talk to me alone about this . . . or if we can all go back to the other room . . . and talk all this out together?” 
“Oh . . . God.” Laurel murmured as she tried to quit crying. “Do we have to?” 
“Yes.” Rowan answered quietly. “You know we do.” He paused. “We’ll all have to discuss it together sooner or later. But if you want to talk to me about it alone first . . . we can do that.” 
“God . . .” Laurel sat up and started to dry her eyes. “I guess I might as well get it over with. Whether I talk to everyone now or later . . . it’s still going to be just as embarrassing. I might as well get it over with.” Laurel paused. “Before we go . . . could I have a hug? I really need one.” 
“Of course.” Rowan took her in his arms, hugging her tightly. “I love you.” He whispered in her ear. 
“OK.” Laurel spoke after a bit, getting up and taking Rowan’s hand. “Let’s go.”
Each of Mabel’s rooms came supplied with two robes. Laurel put on one, while Maynard put on the other. The three of them looked a bit strange walking down the corridors in identical robes, but it was still better than trying to wear their outfits from the night before. 
Rowan’s key led the three of them through all the twists and turns and changes of corridors that led back to the room he and Amelia had been sharing. When Rowan opened the door and the three of them entered, Amelia quickly came over and put an arm around Laurel. 
“Rowan.” Amelia spoke quietly. “Why don’t you and Maynard get us each a big mug of hot chocolate? I think a hot drink would be nice right now.” 
“How does that sound to you, Laurel?” Rowan asked her. “Would you like something hot to drink?” When Laurel nodded her head “yes”, he and Maynard left to get the drinks. 
“Laurel.” Amelia spoke softly to her. “Would you like to wash your face or anything? I could come and help.” 
“OK.” Laurel said with a small frown. “I bet I look a mess.” 
“Only just a bit around the edges.” Amelia replied quietly. 
“I’m just going to help Laurel wash her face.” Amelia called out as she walked with Laurel to the bathroom. “We’ll be right out.” 
Amelia got a washcloth and ran some warm water on it. After Laurel had washed her face, she closed the lid to the toilet and sat down with her head in her hands. 
“How can I go out there . . . and discuss all this?” She asked Amelia helplessly. “I’ve made a giant mess of everyone’s night.” 
“No . . . you haven’t.” Amelia sat down on the edge of the giant tub across from her. She reached over and got a brush and began to brush some of the tangles from Laurel’s long hair. Amelia was dressed in an identical robe to everyone else’s and she gathered it around herself to stand and brush the back of Laurel’s hair. 
“Actually . . . I think it’s a good thing this happened.” Amelia commented as she gently brushed Laurel’s hair.
“You do?” Laurel said in a surprised voice. “But . . . I’ve spoiled your evening with Rowan. And . . . I’ve definitely spoiled Maynard’s evening with me.” 
“Will you please quit worrying about spoiling everyone’s evening?” Amelia spoke softly. “I’m glad Maynard contacted me. If you had had to spend the rest of the night in this state . . . you would’ve been a basket case by morning and all of us would’ve wished you had interrupted us sooner.” 
“Now.” Amelia sat back down on the edge of the tub. “That’s much better. Let’s go out and talk this whole thing out.” 
“God . . . do I have to?” Laurel asked hesitantly, and then she paused. “Never mind . . . don’t answer that. I know I do.” Laurel sent a small smile in Amelia’s direction. “Thanks for brushing my hair . . . I don’t feel quite so dilapidated now.” 
“Good.” Amelia smiled at her. “Let’s go talk.” 
When Amelia and Laurel returned to the main room, they found Rowan and Maynard sitting on the couch. Rowan and Maynard were each sipping a mug of hot chocolate and there were two more mugs on the crystal table. Laurel reached down and collected hers, settling herself down on the couch beside Rowan. As Laurel’s eyes roamed the elegant room, she couldn’t help but notice Amelia and Rowan’s clothes lying neatly over one of the nearby chairs. Laurel glanced in Amelia’s direction. Amelia must have thoughtfully picked up the room before the three of them arrived. When Laurel also saw that the huge elegant canopy bed had also been neatly made, it only confirmed Amelia’s thoughtfulness. 
Amelia picked up her drink and sat down in one of the leather chairs facing the couch from across the crystal table, curling her feet up under herself. 
“Well . . .” Rowan looked carefully at Laurel. “Are you feeling any better?”
“Yes.” Laurel answered quietly. “I just feel like a giant fool . . . the way I’ve managed to make a mess of things.” 
“No . . . you haven’t.” Amelia smiled at Laurel. “And . . . now that we’re all gathered in one place . . . I’ll explain why.” 
Amelia leaned back in her chair and took a small sip of her drink. 
“I think I’ve figured out . . . why Laurel is so upset.” Amelia sent another small smile in Laurel’s direction. “And . . . if Laurel doesn’t mind . . . I’ll just ask her a few questions to make sure I’m right.” Amelia watched Laurel carefully. 
“I think if I ask a few questions . . . we’ll be able to get to the heart of the problem faster. Is that all right with you?” Amelia asked Laurel gently.
“OK.” Laurel answered a bit doubtfully. 
“Good.” Amelia smiled encouragingly at her. “First . . . before I start . . . I need to make sure that you’ll think very carefully before you answer. And then . . . when you answer . . . that you’ll answer as truthfully as you can. Can you do that, Laurel?” Amelia looked at Laurel and received a small nod from her. 
Without another word, Amelia put her drink down and got up. She walked around the crystal table and leaned down and gently kissed Rowan. Rowan looked at Amelia in surprise, as did Laurel and Maynard. It seemed an unlikely thing for her to do. 
“Laurel, does it bother you that I just kissed Rowan?” Amelia asked carefully, after returning to her chair and curling her legs under herself once again. 
“No.” Laurel answered slowly, after thinking about Amelia’s question for a few moments. “I’m surprised that you chose that moment to kiss Rowan. But . . . I don’t mind.” A small smile lit Laurel’s eyes. “How can I mind? I know he’s lots of fun to kiss.” 
“Good.” Amelia smiled encouragingly at Laurel. “I didn’t think you’d mind. Now let’s move on to the next question. This one will be a bit more difficult . . . but don’t forget to think carefully before you answer.” Amelia paused and watched Laurel’s face carefully. “Does it bother you that Rowan and I made love tonight?” 
Rowan shot a look in Amelia’s direction. He hoped to God Amelia knew what she was doing. Then he turned to watch Laurel’s face carefully as she thought about her answer. Maynard sat next to Rowan on the couch and sipped slowly at his drink. He watched Laurel, too. Maynard knew that Laurel would have the guts to answer the question. Only recently, he’d seen her in situations that called for just this same type of courage. 
“Well . . .” Laurel answered slowly after carefully thinking about the question. “It doesn’t really bother me.” Laurel paused. “It just makes me feel a bit strange inside to realize that you and Rowan made love . . . that something very important has happened in his life and I have no part in it. I don’t know about it. I didn’t share in it.” She paused again. “It’s like there’s a part of him now that I know nothing about . . . that I can’t know about because it involves the two of you.” 
“When you were upset tonight . . . what was the most upsetting thing to you right then? What upset you the most?” Amelia went on quietly. 
“Well . . . let me think.” Laurel pondered aloud. “I kept thinking about what you and Rowan were doing in here. As much as I tried not to think about it . . . I still kept wondering what was going on.” She paused. “Then . . . I remember feeling left out.” She frowned. “Then . . . I wanted a hug from Rowan. He had said that whenever I needed a hug . . . I could get one. But . . . I couldn’t get a hug from Rowan . . . because I wouldn’t be able to see him until morning. And while I waited for morning to come . . . the time went by so slowly. As hard as I tried not to . . . all I seemed to do was think about you and Rowan in here . . . and what you might be doing.” Laurel looked at Amelia. “So . . . I guess the thing that upset me the most . . . was the fact that when I wanted Rowan . . . when I needed to talk to him and get a hug . . . I couldn’t . . . because I had to wait until morning.” 
“Well . . . that’s it.” Amelia spoke with a smile on her face. “That’s the problem.” 
The others around the table looked at Amelia with questioning looks. 
“Let me see if I can clarify it.” Amelia went on slowly, after seeing their faces. “As long as Laurel has known Rowan . . . she’s been able to be with him whenever she wanted. If he wasn’t physically in her presence, she could always go to wherever he was if she wanted. If Rowan was working in Lab, she could go there and talk to him. If he was in Recon and Laurel wasn’t, she could still contact him and talk to him.” Amelia looked around at the others who seemed to be listening intently to her every word. 
“Tonight was the first time Laurel has ever been totally separated from Rowan . . . unable to contact him at all. And Laurel felt . . . as she has every right to feel . . . that she had been banished from his presence.” Amelia looked carefully at Laurel. “No wonder you were so upset. I would be, too. If I needed Rowan or you or Maynard as badly as you needed Rowan . . . and found myself unable to even contact you in any way . . . I’d be very upset, too.”
Amelia got up and walked over to Laurel, sitting down on the couch beside her. 
“I’m ever so sorry . . . Laurel.” Amelia spoke softly, looking into her eyes. “I only meant to share Rowan with you. I never meant to take him away from you . . . even for one night.” 
Laurel leaned back into the couch and thought very carefully about what Amelia had said. 
“You’re right.” Laurel eventually said in a quiet voice. “I was upset about not being able to be with Rowan right then . . . not about the fact that you were making love to him.” Laurel turned and smiled at Rowan. “Knowing that you and Amelia were making love was strange . . . but I didn’t get upset until I realized that I’d have to wait until morning to talk to you . . . or touch you . . . or get a hug from you. You were locked away from me until morning. That’s what upset me.” 
“I’m beginning to understand.” Rowan said thoughtfully. “When you made love with Maynard . . . it was sort of the same for me. It felt very strange to know that you were making love to another man. But what bothered me the most . . . was wondering and worrying if you were all right.” Rowan glanced apologetically at Maynard. “I know how strong AIs are and I was worried about Laurel.” 
“Laurel felt banished from you . . . separated from you.” Amelia returned to her chair, and looked at Rowan.  “She felt left out. Something was happening to you . . . in this case something very important to you . . . that she couldn’t share in . . . couldn’t even know about.” Amelia explained quietly. “You and Laurel are so used to sharing everything that happens in each other’s lives that the separation from what is happening to the other person . . . is more upsetting to you than what the other person is doing.” Amelia watched Rowan’s face as she talked. “You two are inseparable. It’s an important aspect of your relationship. When a person shares everything with another person they stay very close. It could be the only way to stay close to another person, as close as you and Laurel have always wanted to be.” 
“So if Maynard and I are going to share your and Laurel’s lives in an intimate fashion as we’ve already decided . . . we’ll have to find a way to include Maynard and I into the inseparable relationship you and Laurel already have.” Amelia paused and looked carefully at Rowan. “Laurel doesn’t mind if you make love to me . . . she just doesn’t want to be excluded from any area of your life.”
“Amelia’s right.” Maynard agreed quietly. “And we all know . . . that sex is an important part of both your lives . . .” Here Maynard paused to wink at Laurel. “So the part of both your lives that you’re finding yourselves separated from . . . is a very important part.”
“Yes, that’s exactly right.” Amelia smiled appreciatively at Maynard. “So we just have to find a way to keep sex from being a part of your lives from which the other one is separated.” 
“Well . . .” Maynard grinned and a gleam lit his eyes. “That’s easy. We all just live in one huge dome . . . and sleep in one huge bed.” He grinned at Laurel. “That way . . . no one can ever feel left out.” 
“Oh . . . God.” Laurel gasped. “Wouldn’t that be very embarrassing?” 
“At first.” Amelia glanced at her. “It would be like the first few dates with a person, when you never want the other person to know that you do any of the basic things in life. But eventually you get comfortable enough around the other person and it doesn’t matter. You begin to be yourself.” Amelia smiled at Laurel. “It would be like that at first. There’s bound to be some real embarrassing moments. But . . . I bet we’d soon all relax into just being ourselves around each other.” 
“Well . . .” Rowan smiled at Amelia. “It certainly eliminates the feeling of being left out. Everyone would know everything that was going on in everyone else’s life.” He grinned. “Talk about growing close.” 
“Does it mean that I’d have to watch when you and Amelia made love?” Laurel asked in a small voice. “Or . . . my God . . . would you and Amelia watch while I made love to Maynard?” Laurel glanced quickly at Maynard. 
“Not unless you happened to want that.” Rowan said in a teasing voice, leaning over to kiss her. “When you come right down to it . . . we’re four very inventive people. I’m sure we can come up with a way to sneak off and have sex if we want to . . . at least until we get comfortable enough to do it in the same room.” 
“Or we could build a special room or two into the dome . . . rooms that could be used to relieve our . . . ah . . . tensions.” Maynard suggested with a smile. “And all the rooms would be suitably soundproofed, of course.”
“Or we could just decide that sex is a natural thing . . . and not worry if someone we care about . . . knows that we’re doing it.” Rowan grinned. “The possibilities are endless.” He laughed. “Challenging . . . but endless.” 
“What do you say?” Amelia asked smiling. “Do we all move in together?” She laughed. “Actually . . . this isn’t that much different from before. When Maynard was a teddy bear and I occupied the ship we all lived together in your dome in a way. Right?”
“That’s right.” Rowan grinned at Amelia, a lusty look in his eye. “The only difference is . . . now we can all share in lots more ways.” 
Rowan glanced at Laurel. He moved closer to her, motioning to Maynard to join him. Maynard came over and sat down on the couch on the other side of Laurel. 
“Laurel.” Rowan spoke softly, taking her hand. “One of the things you like most is how we snuggle in bed . . . how nice it feels to go to sleep in each other’s arms and wake up next to each other.” Laurel smiled at Rowan and Rowan nodded towards Maynard. “Wouldn’t that pleasure and closeness be even nicer with two men to hold you while you slept?” 
“Rowan’s right.” Maynard reached for Laurel’s other hand and held it gently in his. He brought her hand to his lips and gently kissed it. “I know already . . . how much you like to have me hold you. Wouldn’t you like for me to be able to hold you every night?” A gleam lit Maynard’s eyes as he grinned at her. “I know it sure would be nice for me.” 
“God . . .” Laurel murmured, a grin lighting her eyes. “You guys are ganging up on me.” She laughed. “But . . . you’re right.” 
“It does sound a bit complicated.” Laurel continued, her face growing a bit serious. “Not the living together part . . . that’s nice and easy. We practically do that already.” She paused. “But the sex part . . . that’s going to take some working out.” 
“So . . . we’ll work it out.” Rowan smiled at Laurel. “I’d much prefer to go through a period of possible embarrassment . . . than to miss sharing even a moment of your life.” He grinned down at her. “Especially a part of your life that is this important to you.” Rowan leaned down and kissed her. “If we’re not actually having sex . . . we’re always either talking about it . . . or thinking about it. Right?” 
“You’re right.” Laurel leaned over to kiss first Rowan and then Maynard. “Well . . . I guess after the way I came apart at the seams tonight . . . you’re all even that much closer to getting to know me. And hopefully . . . a large part of my embarrassment is behind me.”
“God.” Laurel looked around at the others. “Is this settled now? I’m starved.” 
Rowan caught looks from Maynard and Amelia. “Yes, all settled.” 
“Good.” Laurel got up and went to the Food Dispenser. “Does anyone else want anything to eat?” 
Rowan requested some tunes from the sound system he had designed into the room. Laurel ordered up cheese blintzes with fresh strawberries all around. The four of them sat and listened to the music and ate. 
“This is really a great room.” Laurel commented as she ate. 
“Yes.” Amelia answered with a smile. “Rowan really did a very nice job designing it.” 
“Maybe we could design something like this for our dome on the ship.” Laurel caught Amelia’s look and smiled.
“I don’t see why not.” Rowan commented quietly. “We’re going to have to redesign our dome anyway. Anyone have any ideas on what we could include in the plans?” He laughed. “Obviously the first step will be to make it bigger.” He paused. “And I’d like a large hot tub . . . out on a deck that faced the beach . . . big enough to hold all of us very comfortably.” 
“That’s a great idea.” Amelia replied with a smile. “I think it would be fun to make one big room the bedroom and make the entire room have a wall-to-wall bed. That way there’d be plenty of room for us all to sleep without anyone getting pushed out of bed. And we could make windows all around the edge at just the right height . . . so that when we lay in bed we can see out.” Amelia smiled. “And at night we can talk and watch the stars for hours.” She smiled at Rowan. 
“That sounds nice.” Maynard smiled. “We’ll also need to design a few more workstations into the dome and I’d like to add a large fireplace to the plans . . . maybe one that would be in a room something like this.” Maynard grinned at first Laurel, and then at Amelia. “And I’d like a couple of big bearskin rugs for right in front of the fireplace. That sounds nice, doesn’t it?” 
Maynard got resounding “Yeses” from everyone. 
While all four of them were cleaning up the food mess and recycling the lot, Rowan pulled Laurel to one side. 
“Are you OK with all this?” Rowan asked concern in his eyes. 
“Yes.” Laurel answered with a smile. “There’s bound to be embarrassing moments at first, but it will be lots of fun.” She reached up and touched Rowan’s cheek gently. “And the best part will be that I won’t have to be separated from any part of your life . . . just like the last umpteen years.” Laurel smiled up into his eyes. “I love you and I want to share all my time with you.” Laurel kissed him. 
“Me, too.” Rowan reached out and pulled Laurel towards him and hugged her. “And I can give you hugs . . . whenever you want them.” 
“Yes.” Laurel smiled. “That’s the best part.” 
The four of them found that they were soon faced with their first slightly embarrassing moment. But they solved it soon enough. They all just dropped their robes on the count of three and jumped into bed. When everyone was settled, they found that Maynard held Laurel and Amelia held Rowan and Laurel and Rowan held each other. 
When Maynard and Amelia were sure that Rowan and Laurel were asleep, they went back to the table to draw up some plans for the dome the four of them would create as soon as they got back to the ship. Amelia and Maynard figured they could get the plans done and climb back into bed in time to be holding Rowan and Laurel when they woke up.

*THE END*
